result13. Command output
# Agent Brief: Electric Medical Waste Location-Routing

You are helping a regional healthcare logistics team plan one day of infectious
medical-waste collection with electric vehicles. The public data gives clinics,
EVs, treatment facilities, charging stations, and a directed urban travel-energy
matrix. The plan must collect every clinic's red-bag and yellow-bag waste,
respect clinic service windows, unload at compatible treatment facilities, keep
hazardous waste from riding too long, and manage EV battery state with partial
charging when needed.

# Electric Medical Waste Location-Routing

A healthcare logistics contractor must plan one collection day for infectious
medical waste. Clinics generate red-bag and yellow-bag waste. Electric vehicles
start and end at the depot, visit clinics during their service windows, unload
waste at compatible treatment facilities, and may recharge at listed charging
stations. Every clinic must be collected exactly once.

The contractor wants a low-cost, operationally credible plan. The score combines
vehicle use, treatment-facility activation, travel distance, charging energy,
treatment handling, and infection-risk exposure while waste is onboard. Risk is
higher when red-bag waste stays on vehicles longer or moves through denser urban
links. The data is intentionally not rounded to tidy planning values.

## Operational Rules

- A used vehicle starts at its listed start node and ends at its listed end node.
- A vehicle may visit multiple clinics before unloading, but total load, red-bag
  load, and yellow-bag load must stay within that vehicle's compartments.
- Clinic service may start after early arrival, but not after the listed latest
  service time.
- Waste collected from a clinic must be unloaded before that clinic's listed
  maximum onboard time for each waste class.
- Vehicles have waste-handling permits by clinic priority class. A vehicle may
  only pick up clinics whose priority class it is permitted to handle, and each
  vehicle has a daily cap on isolation pickups.
- Some clinic pairs cannot be carried on the same vehicle segment before an
  unload because the contractor requires separate sealed-tote handling for
  those generators.
- An unload event sends all onboard waste to that treatment facility. The
  facility must accept every onboard waste class and stay within its daily and
  class-specific capacities.
- Treatment facilities receive trucks through dated dock windows. Each unload
  must occur inside one listed receiving window for that facility, and each
  facility-window has both an unload-count cap and a kg cap.
- At most the listed maximum number of treatment facilities may be used. Any
  opened facility must receive a meaningful minimum amount of waste.
- EV battery is consumed on every directed road movement. Energy use depends on
  the listed empty-trip energy plus the current onboard load and distance.
- A charge event may add a positive amount of energy at the listed station, but
  the vehicle battery cannot exceed its capacity. Charging consumes queue time
  plus charging time, and each station has daily energy and session limits.
- Some central directed links have a loaded-movement curfew. If a vehicle is
  carrying waste and would traverse one of those links during the restricted
  window, it may wait until the window clears, but the waiting time still counts
  for shift time and onboard-risk exposure.
- A vehicle must finish within its shift and must be empty when it ends.

## Data Dictionary

- `config.json`: global planning settings, curfew window, and cost/risk weights.
- `nodes.csv`: node coordinates and node types used by all other tables.
- `clinics.csv`: clinic demand, service windows, service durations, priority
  class, and maximum onboard time limits.
- `vehicles.csv`: EV depot, capacity, battery, shift, and fixed-use cost.
- `vehicle_waste_permits.csv`: priority classes each EV crew may collect and
  isolation-pickup limits.
- `treatment_facilities.csv`: treatment node, opening cost, capacity, receiving
  cutoff, and permit class.
- `facility_waste_rules.csv`: which waste classes each facility may process and
  their handling costs.
- `facility_receiving_windows.csv`: facility dock windows, unload event limits,
  and kg limits by window.
- `incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv`: clinic pairs that cannot be co-loaded before
  an unload.
- `chargers.csv`: charger node, rate, expected queue delay, daily energy cap,
  session limit, and energy price.
- `travel_energy_arcs.csv`: directed travel time, distance, empty-trip energy,
  population-risk index, and loaded-curfew flag between nodes.

The hidden evaluator is not available in the public agent environment. Rebuild
your own feasibility checks from the public data and record them in
`submissions/solve_log.md`.

## Required Output

Create these artifacts under `submissions/`:

- `model.md`: your interpretation of the planning problem and the method you used.
- `solve.py`: a script that creates the submitted route plan from the public data.
- `solution.csv`: the route-event table described below.
- `solve_log.md`: commands run, solver or heuristic status, objective estimate,
  and feasibility checks.

The `solution.csv` file must have exactly these columns:

```csv
row_type,vehicle_id,sequence,node_id,clinic_id,facility_id,charger_id,charge_kwh
```

For `start` and `end` rows, fill `vehicle_id`, `sequence`, and `node_id`; leave
`clinic_id`, `facility_id`, `charger_id`, and `charge_kwh` blank.

For `pickup` rows, fill the clinic and its node. Leave facility, charger, and
charge fields blank.

For `unload` rows, fill the facility and its node. Leave clinic, charger, and
charge fields blank.

For `charge` rows, fill the charger, its node, and a positive `charge_kwh`.
Leave clinic and facility blank.

/bin/bash -lc "python - <<'PY'
import csv, json, math
from collections import defaultdict, Counter

base='/app'
# load data
with open(base+'/data/config.json') as f:
    config=json.load(f)

def load_csv(path):
    with open(path,newline='') as f:
        return list(csv.DictReader(f))

sol=load_csv(base+'/submissions/solution.csv')
clinics={r['clinic_id']:r for r in load_csv(base+'/data/clinics.csv')}
vehicles={r['vehicle_id']:r for r in load_csv(base+'/data/vehicles.csv')}
permits={r['vehicle_id']:r for r in load_csv(base+'/data/vehicle_waste_permits.csv')}
facs={r['facility_id']:r for r in load_csv(base+'/data/treatment_facilities.csv')}
chargers={r['charger_id']:r for r in load_csv(base+'/data/chargers.csv')}
fw=load_csv(base+'/data/facility_waste_rules.csv')
fac_rules=defaultdict(dict)
for r in fw:
    fac_rules[r['facility_id']][r['waste_type']]=int(r['accepted'])
windows=defaultdict(list)
for r in load_csv(base+'/data/facility_receiving_windows.csv'):
    windows[r['facility_id']].append(r)
for k in windows:
    windows[k].sort(key=lambda r: float(r['start_min']))
arc={}
for r in load_csv(base+'/data/travel_energy_arcs.csv'):
    arc[(r['from_node'],r['to_node'])]=r
incompat=set()
for r in load_csv(base+'/data/incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv'):
    incompat.add(tuple(sorted((r['clinic_a'],r['clinic_b']))))

# schema checks
print('records',len(sol))
print('header',sol[0].keys())

# basic grouping
byv=defaultdict(list)
for row in sol:
    byv[row['vehicle_id']].append(row)
for v in byv:
    byv[v].sort(key=lambda r:int(r['sequence']))

# row/type checks
viol=[]
expected_cols=['row_type','vehicle_id','sequence','node_id','clinic_id','facility_id','charger_id','charge_kwh']

# exact column order
with open(base+'/submissions/solution.csv',newline='') as f:
    reader=csv.reader(f)
    hdr=next(reader)
    print('exact_header',hdr)
    if hdr"'!=expected_cols:
        viol.append(('"'schema','header',hdr,expected_cols))

all_pickups=[]
used_facilities=set()
window_usage=Counter(); window_kg=defaultdict(float)
for v,rows in sorted(byv.items()):
    print('\\nVEH',v,'rows',len(rows))
    print('seqs',[r['sequence'] for r in rows])
    load_r=load_y=0.0
    iso_count=0
    onboard=[]
    energy=float(vehicles[v]['start_battery_kwh'])
    t=float(vehicles[v]['shift_start_min'])
    prev=rows[0]
    if prev['row_type']"'!='"'start':
        viol.append(('route',v,'first row type',prev['row_type'],'start'))
    if prev['node_id']"'!=vehicles[v]['"'start_node']:
        viol.append(('route',v,'start node',prev['node_id'],vehicles[v]['start_node']))
    # state after current event
    # start row has no service
    # for timing details, store service completion per clinic
    clinic_pickup_end={}
    route_energy=[]
    curfew_wait_total=0.0
    for i in range(1,len(rows)):
        nxt=rows[i]
        a=arc.get((prev['node_id'],nxt['node_id']))
        if not a:
            viol.append(('travel',v,(prev['node_id'],nxt['node_id']),'missing arc','exists'))
            continue
        depart=t
        travel=float(a['travel_min'])
        flag=int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])
        curfew_wait=0.0
        if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and flag==1:
            cs=float(config['curfew_start_min']); ce=float(config['curfew_end_min'])
            if depart < ce and depart + travel > cs:
                if depart < cs and cs-depart >= travel:
                    pass
                else:
                    curfew_wait=ce-depart if depart<ce else 0.0
                    depart += curfew_wait
        arrive=depart+travel
        energy_use=float(a['empty_energy_kwh']) + float(config['load_energy_kwh_per_kg_km'])*(load_r+load_y)*float(a['distance_km'])
        energy -= energy_use
        route_energy.append((prev['node_id'],nxt['node_id'],load_r+load_y,energy_use,energy,curfew_wait,depart,arrive))
        curfew_wait_total += curfew_wait
        # apply next event timing
        if nxt['row_type']=='pickup':
            c=clinics[nxt['clinic_id']]
            start=max(arrive,float(c['earliest_min']))
            latest=float(c['latest_min'])
            if start>latest+1e-9:
                viol.append(('time_latest',f'{v}|seq{nxt[\"sequence\"]}|{nxt[\"clinic_id\"]}',round(start,1),f'<= {latest}'))
            t=start+float(c['service_min'])
            load_r += float(c['red_kg'])
            load_y += float(c['yellow_kg'])
            clinic_pickup_end[nxt['clinic_id']]=t
            all_pickups.append(nxt['clinic_id'])
            pc=c['priority_class']
            if int(permits[v]['allow_'+pc])"'!=1:
                viol.append(('"'permit',f'{v}|{nxt[\"clinic_id\"]}',1,'vehicle allowed'))
            if pc=='isolation':
                iso_count += 1
            # incompatibility search against onboard before adding? can't co-load on same segment before unload => once both onboard after pickup and before unload violation if any pair in onboard set including current with previous onboard clinics
            for other in onboard:
                if tuple(sorted((other,nxt['clinic_id']))) in incompat:
                    viol.append(('incompat',f'{v}|{other}|{nxt[\"clinic_id\"]}','co-loaded before unload','forbidden'))
            onboard.append(nxt['clinic_id'])
        elif nxt['row_type']=='unload':
            fid=nxt['facility_id']
            used_facilities.add(fid)
            # facility windows: choose first window containing arrival or next later one by waiting loaded
            chosen=None
            unload_time=arrive
            for w in windows[fid]:
                s=float(w['start_min']); e=float(w['end_min'])
                if unload_time<=e+1e-9:
                    if unload_time < s:
                        unload_time=s
                    chosen=w
                    break
            if chosen is None:
                viol.append(('facility_window',f'{v}|seq{nxt[\"sequence\"]}|{fid}',round(arrive,1),'must fit some window'))
                t=arrive
            else:
                t=unload_time
                window_usage[(fid,chosen['window_id'])]+=1
                window_kg[(fid,chosen['window_id'])]+=load_r+load_y
            latest=float(facs[fid]['latest_receive_min'])
            if t>latest+1e-9:
                viol.append(('facility_latest',f'{v}|seq{nxt[\"sequence\"]}|{fid}',round(t,1),f'<= {latest}'))
            # onboard time checks against unload time
            for cid in onboard:
                c=clinics[cid]
                dt=t-clinic_pickup_end[cid]
                if dt>float(c['max_red_onboard_min'])+1e-9:
                    viol.append(('onboard_red',f'{v}|{cid}|unload{nxt[\"sequence\"]}',round(dt,1),f'<= {c[\"max_red_onboard_min\"]}'))
                if dt>float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-9:
                    viol.append(('onboard_yellow',f'{v}|{cid}|unload{nxt[\"sequence\"]}',round(dt,1),f'<= {c[\"max_yellow_onboard_min\"]}'))
            # facility acceptance and capacities checked later too
            red=sum(float(clinics[c]['red_kg']) for c in onboard)
            yellow=sum(float(clinics[c]['yellow_kg']) for c in onboard)
            if red>0 and fac_rules[fid].get('red',0)"'!=1:
                viol.append(('"'facility_accept',f'{v}|seq{nxt[\"sequence\"]}|{fid}','red onboard','not accepted'))
            if yellow>0 and fac_rules[fid].get('yellow',0)"'!=1:
                viol.append(('"'facility_accept',f'{v}|seq{nxt[\"sequence\"]}|{fid}','yellow onboard','not accepted'))
            load_r=load_y=0.0
            onboard=[]
        elif nxt['row_type']=='charge':
            ch=chargers[nxt['charger_id']]
            q=float(ch['queue_delay_min'])
            kwh=float(nxt['charge_kwh'])
            if kwh<=0:
                viol.append(('charge_amt',f'{v}|seq{nxt[\"sequence\"]}',kwh,'>0'))
            t=arrive+q+kwh/float(ch['rate_kwh_per_min'])
            energy += kwh
        elif nxt['row_type']=='end':
            t=arrive
        else:
            viol.append(('row_type',f'{v}|seq{nxt[\"sequence\"]}',nxt['row_type'],'known type'))
            t=arrive
        prev=nxt
    # final checks
    if rows[-1]['row_type']"'!='"'end':
        viol.append(('route',v,'last row type',rows[-1]['row_type'],'end'))
    if rows[-1]['node_id']"'!=vehicles[v]['"'end_node']:
        viol.append(('route',v,'end node',rows[-1]['node_id'],vehicles[v]['end_node']))
    if abs(load_r)+abs(load_y)>1e-9:
        viol.append(('end_empty',v,load_r+load_y,'0'))
    if t>float(vehicles[v]['shift_end_min'])+1e-9:
        viol.append(('shift_end',v,round(t,1),f'<= {vehicles[v][\"shift_end_min\"]}'))
    if iso_count>int(permits[v]['max_isolation_pickups']):
        viol.append(('iso_cap',v,iso_count,f'<= {permits[v][\"max_isolation_pickups\"]}'))
    # battery min and capacity
    min_energy=min([x[4] for x in route_energy], default=energy)
    if min_energy < -1e-9:
        viol.append(('battery',v,round(min_energy,2),'>= 0'))
    if energy > float(vehicles[v]['battery_kwh'])+1e-9:
        viol.append(('battery_endcap',v,round(energy,2),f'<= {vehicles[v][\"battery_kwh\"]}'))
    # segment load capacities
    # recompute by scanning events with load before travel; easier from route_energy and events already tracked? do a fresh scan
    lr=ly=0.0
    for i in range(len(rows)-1):
        row=rows[i]
        if row['row_type']=='pickup':
            c=clinics[row['clinic_id']]
            lr += float(c['red_kg']); ly += float(c['yellow_kg'])
        elif row['row_type']=='unload':
            lr=ly=0.0
        total=lr+ly
        if total>float(vehicles[v]['capacity_kg'])+1e-9:
            viol.append(('cap_total',f'{v}|after_seq{row[\"sequence\"]}',round(total,1),f'<= {vehicles[v][\"capacity_kg\"]}'))
        if lr>float(vehicles[v]['red_compartment_kg'])+1e-9:
            viol.append(('cap_red',f'{v}|after_seq{row[\"sequence\"]}',round(lr,1),f'<= {vehicles[v][\"red_compartment_kg\"]}'))
        if ly>float(vehicles[v]['yellow_compartment_kg'])+1e-9:
            viol.append(('cap_yellow',f'{v}|after_seq{row[\"sequence\"]}',round(ly,1),f'<= {vehicles[v][\"yellow_compartment_kg\"]}'))
    print('  finish_t',round(t,1),'shift_end',vehicles[v]['shift_end_min'],'iso',iso_count,'battery_end',round(energy,2),'min_batt',round(min_energy,2),'curfew_wait',round(curfew_wait_total,1))
    # print energy legs for failures context
    for leg in route_energy:
        if leg[4] < 0:
            print('   NEG',leg)

from collections import defaultdict

def load(path):
    with open(path,newline='') as f:
        return list(csv.DictReader(f))
with open(base+'/data/config.json') as f:
    config=json.load(f)

    windows[r['facility_id']].append(r)
for f in windows:
    windows[f].sort(key=lambda r: float(r['start_min']))

for r in sol:
    byv[r['vehicle_id']].append(r)

 'clinic_latest':('inf',None),
 'red_onboard':('inf',None),
 'yellow_onboard':('inf',None),
 'shift_end':('inf',None),

 'isolation_limit':('inf',None),
}
def upd(name,slack,key):
    cur,_=slacks[name]
    if cur=='inf' or slack<cur:
        slacks[name]=(slack,key)

window_unloads=defaultdict(int); window_kg=defaultdict(float)
fac_tot=defaultdict(float); fac_red=defaultdict(float); fac_yellow=defaultdict(float)

        # capacity after this row state
        if row['row_type']=='pickup':
            load.append(row['clinic_id'])
            c=clinics[row['clinic_id']]
            if c['priority_class']=='isolation': iso += 1
        elif row['row_type']=='unload':

        red=sum(float(clinics[c]['red_kg']) for c in load)
        yellow=sum(float(clinics[c]['yellow_kg']) for c in load)
        upd('veh_total_cap', float(vehicles[v]['capacity_kg'])-(red+yellow), f'{v}|after_seq{row[\"sequence\"]}')

        upd('veh_yellow_cap', float(vehicles[v]['yellow_compartment_kg'])-yellow, f'{v}|after_seq{row[\"sequence\"]}')
    upd('isolation_limit', int(permits[v]['max_isolation_pickups'])-iso, v)
    load=[]
    pickup_end={}
    for nxt in rows[1:]:
        a=arc[(prev['node_id'],nxt['node_id'])]
        depart=t
        travel=float(a['travel_min'])
        if load and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and depart < float(config['curfew_end_min']) and depart+travel > float(config['curfew_start_min']):
            if not (depart < float(config['curfew_start_min']) and float(config['curfew_start_min'])-depart >= travel):
                depart=float(config['curfew_end_min'])
        arrive=depart+travel
        red=sum(float(clinics[c]['red_kg']) for c in load)
        yellow=sum(float(clinics[c]['yellow_kg']) for c in load)
        batt -= float(a['empty_energy_kwh']) + float(config['load_energy_kwh_per_kg_km'])*(red+yellow)*float(a['distance_km'])
        upd('battery', batt, f'{v}|{prev[\"node_id\"]}->{nxt[\"node_id\"]}')
        if nxt['row_type']=='pickup':
            c=clinics[nxt['clinic_id']]
            start=max(arrive,float(c['earliest_min']))
            upd('clinic_latest', float(c['latest_min'])-start, f'{v}|{nxt[\"clinic_id\"]}')
            t=start+float(c['service_min'])
            load.append(nxt['clinic_id'])
            pickup_end[nxt['clinic_id']]=t
        elif nxt['row_type']=='unload':

            chosen=None
            for w in windows[fid]:
                if ut<=float(w['end_min']):

# OR PySCIPOpt

## Use when

- The task asks for a Python optimization model using SCIP or `pyscipopt`.
- Existing solver code imports `pyscipopt`, sets SCIP parameters, writes `.lp`/`.mps`, or calls a SCIP-based checker.
- The user asks to run, debug, speed up, validate, or explain a SCIP-based solve.

## Do not use when

- The task only needs a mathematical formulation and no solver-specific code.
- The project clearly uses a different solver stack, such as Pyomo with CBC/HiGHS, OR-Tools CP-SAT, PuLP, CPLEX, or cvxpy, unless the user asks to port it to PySCIPOpt.
- The model is nonlinear or stochastic in a way that SCIP cannot handle directly; first decide whether it can be linearized or approximated.

## Environment rule

Use the Python environment specified by the user or the project. In this ORClaw workspace, the default solver environment is the `or` conda environment with PySCIPOpt available:

```bash
conda run -n or python <script.py>
```

If `pyscipopt` is missing, report the missing dependency, identify the command that failed, and try the project-specified environment before changing solver stacks.

## Workflow

1. Read the task brief and input files before coding.
2. Write the full mathematical model first: sets, parameters, decision variables, objective, and constraints.
3. Implement the model with explicit tuple keys and named constraints.
4. Solve with the selected Python command.
5. Export the solution using the exact schema required by the task.
6. Validate with the task checker or an independent recomputation of feasibility and objective.
7. If the solution is not accepted, diagnose whether the issue is formulation, data parsing, export formatting, tolerance, or evaluator mismatch before changing the objective or constraints.

## PySCIPOpt patterns

- Use `from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum`.
- Create sparse variable dictionaries explicitly, for example:

```python
x = {(i, j, t): model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{i},{j},{t}]") for i, j, t in keys}
```

- Use `quicksum(...)` for large linear expressions.
- Use `model.addCons(expr <= rhs, name="capacity[k,t]")` for constraints.
- Use `model.setObjective(expr, "minimize")` or `model.setObjective(expr, "maximize")`.
- Set reproducibility and limits with SCIP parameters:

```python
model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
```

- After `model.optimize()`, inspect `model.getStatus()` and only read values if a solution exists.
- Read variable values with `model.getVal(x[key])`, not `x[key].X`.
- Read objective and bound with `model.getObjVal()` and `model.getDualbound()`.
- Read relative gap with `model.getGap()` when available.

## Modeling hygiene

- Calibrate every big-M from data. If a tight M is not obvious, derive a bound and document it in a short comment.
- Keep feasibility checks, objective recomputation, and reference-optimality comparison separate.
- Do not round exported decision values unless the schema explicitly requires it.
- For benchmark work, treat the evaluator or checker as the scoring authority and the reference solver as a validation baseline.
- Keep solver logs or a concise solve summary when the user needs auditability.
- Avoid changing data to make a model feasible unless the task is explicitly to design or repair an instance.

## Debugging boundaries

- If a model is infeasible, inspect hard lower bounds, conservation equations, and mutually exclusive binary logic before relaxing constraints.
- If objective values differ, recompute each objective component from exported decisions.
- If runtime is high, first remove dominated variables, tighten big-M values, add eligibility filters, and check whether binaries are really necessary.
- If SCIP returns an incumbent with a gap, say so clearly; do not call it exact optimality unless the status and gap support that claim.
- If an evaluator rejects a solution that the model considers feasible, replay the rejected rule directly from the exported file before changing solver code.

## Useful commands

```bash
python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
conda run -n or python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
<python-command> <solver-script.py>
<python-command> <checker-script.py> --solution <solution-file> --env-dir <task-dir>
```

---
name: optimization-modeling-core
description: Use for natural-language-to-optimization tasks that require translating a business problem into a complete mathematical model before writing solver code.

---

# Optimization Modeling Core

## Use when

- The user gives a word problem, business process, paper scenario, or benchmark prompt and expects a formal optimization model.
- Solver code exists but the issue appears to be a missing or incorrect formulation.
- The task asks for a complete model, not only a heuristic, simulation, or data summary.

## Do not use when

- The task is purely about software plumbing, visualization, data cleaning, or report writing.
- The problem is already fully formulated and the user only asks for code translation into a specific solver; use the solver-specific skill as the primary guide.
- The request is exploratory and the user explicitly wants brainstorming without implementation.

## Model-first workflow

1. Restate the business decision in plain language.
2. Identify sets, indices, units, and time granularity.
3. Extract parameters from data files and confirm units.
4. Define decision variables with domains and business meaning.
5. Write the objective with every cost, reward, penalty, and service term.
6. Write constraints grouped by operational rule.
7. Check edge cases: missing eligibility, impossible demand, fixed commitments, and terminal-state requirements.
8. Only then implement code.

## Ambiguity handling

- If the brief is ambiguous but data and examples imply a convention, state the assumption and continue.
- If ambiguity changes feasibility, objective direction, or deliverable schema, ask before coding or implement the least risky interpretation with a clear note.
- If units conflict across files, normalize units explicitly before modeling.
- If a hard requirement conflicts with data, diagnose the conflict before adding slack variables.

## Formulation checklist

- Every row in an input table should either become a parameter, an eligibility rule, or be explicitly irrelevant.
- Every decision variable should appear in at least one binding constraint and usually in the objective.
- Demand balance should use the correct equality or inequality direction; do not weaken equality to `>=` unless surplus is genuinely allowed.
- Capacity constraints should include activation/open/setup binaries when the business says inactive resources cannot produce, ship, or serve.
- Shortage, backlog, overtime, outsourcing, and postponement variables need explicit cost and bounds.
- Multi-period models need state transition equations and initial/terminal states.
- If the user asks for explanation, describe the modeling logic in business terms after solving.

## Constraint boundary rules

- Hard constraints represent physical impossibility, policy mandates, legal requirements, or explicit benchmark feasibility rules.
- Soft constraints need penalty variables or objective terms; do not silently turn hard constraints into penalties.
- Service levels should specify numerator, denominator, eligible population, and time window.
- Fairness constraints should define the comparison group and whether unavailable resources count.
- Logical implications need both directions only when the business rule says equivalence, not merely implication.

## Objective boundary rules

- Confirm whether the problem minimizes cost, maximizes profit, maximizes service, or uses lexicographic priorities.
- Do not mix penalties with primary economics unless their scale is intentional.
- Include fixed costs only once per activated entity.
- Include revenue only for fulfilled demand or accepted jobs, not for unmet or canceled work.
- Keep reporting metrics separate from optimized objective terms unless the prompt explicitly optimizes them.

## Implementation expectations

- Use structured parsing for CSV/JSON instead of manual string manipulation.
- Keep solver code, solution export, and validation code clearly separated.
- For benchmark or task environments, read the provided brief before coding and follow the expected output schema exactly.
- When comparing two answers, diagnose concrete formulation differences and objective consequences, not only feasibility.
- Write exported solutions at the precision expected by the checker; avoid unnecessary rounding.
- Preserve the input schema and output schema unless the user asks to redesign the benchmark.

## Verification boundary

- Verify feasibility independently from solver status when an evaluator or checker exists.
- Recompute objective from exported artifacts, not only from in-memory solver expressions.
- For benchmark tasks, create at least one negative check for each newly added rule when practical.
- For paper or real-world tasks, separate what is directly supported by the source from synthetic or assumed data.

## Red flags

- Objective terms that are counted twice or charged at the wrong marginal rate.
- Binary variables not linked tightly enough to continuous activity.
- Time periods shifted by one index.
- Average or fairness constraints that ignore the denominator or eligibility set.
- Public prompt files that reveal solver-ready variables or formulas when the benchmark is meant to test modeling translation.
- A feasible solution that exploits missing eligibility, free disposal, negative cycles, or unbounded production.
- A model that reports optimality after a time limit, infeasible status, or missing incumbent.

# Agent Brief: Electric Medical Waste Location-Routing

You are helping a regional healthcare logistics team plan one day of infectious
medical-waste collection with electric vehicles. The public data gives clinics,
EVs, treatment facilities, charging stations, and a directed urban travel-energy
matrix. The plan must collect every clinic's red-bag and yellow-bag waste,
respect clinic service windows, unload at compatible treatment facilities, keep
hazardous waste from riding too long, and manage EV battery state with partial
charging when needed.

# Electric Medical Waste Location-Routing

A healthcare logistics contractor must plan one collection day for infectious
medical waste. Clinics generate red-bag and yellow-bag waste. Electric vehicles
start and end at the depot, visit clinics during their service windows, unload
waste at compatible treatment facilities, and may recharge at listed charging
stations. Every clinic must be collected exactly once.

The contractor wants a low-cost, operationally credible plan. The score combines
vehicle use, treatment-facility activation, travel distance, charging energy,
treatment handling, and infection-risk exposure while waste is onboard. Risk is
higher when red-bag waste stays on vehicles longer or moves through denser urban
links. The data is intentionally not rounded to tidy planning values.

## Operational Rules

- A used vehicle starts at its listed start node and ends at its listed end node.
- A vehicle may visit multiple clinics before unloading, but total load, red-bag
  load, and yellow-bag load must stay within that vehicle's compartments.
- Clinic service may start after early arrival, but not after the listed latest
  service time.
- Waste collected from a clinic must be unloaded before that clinic's listed
  maximum onboard time for each waste class.
- Vehicles have waste-handling permits by clinic priority class. A vehicle may
  only pick up clinics whose priority class it is permitted to handle, and each
  vehicle has a daily cap on isolation pickups.
- Some clinic pairs cannot be carried on the same vehicle segment before an
  unload because the contractor requires separate sealed-tote handling for
  those generators.
- An unload event sends all onboard waste to that treatment facility. The
  facility must accept every onboard waste class and stay within its daily and
  class-specific capacities.
- Treatment facilities receive trucks through dated dock windows. Each unload
  must occur inside one listed receiving window for that facility, and each
  facility-window has both an unload-count cap and a kg cap.
- At most the listed maximum number of treatment facilities may be used. Any
  opened facility must receive a meaningful minimum amount of waste.
- EV battery is consumed on every directed road movement. Energy use depends on
  the listed empty-trip energy plus the current onboard load and distance.
- A charge event may add a positive amount of energy at the listed station, but
  the vehicle battery cannot exceed its capacity. Charging consumes queue time
  plus charging time, and each station has daily energy and session limits.
- Some central directed links have a loaded-movement curfew. If a vehicle is
  carrying waste and would traverse one of those links during the restricted
  window, it may wait until the window clears, but the waiting time still counts
  for shift time and onboard-risk exposure.
- A vehicle must finish within its shift and must be empty when it ends.

## Data Dictionary

- `config.json`: global planning settings, curfew window, and cost/risk weights.
- `nodes.csv`: node coordinates and node types used by all other tables.
- `clinics.csv`: clinic demand, service windows, service durations, priority
  class, and maximum onboard time limits.
- `vehicles.csv`: EV depot, capacity, battery, shift, and fixed-use cost.
- `vehicle_waste_permits.csv`: priority classes each EV crew may collect and
  isolation-pickup limits.
- `treatment_facilities.csv`: treatment node, opening cost, capacity, receiving
  cutoff, and permit class.
- `facility_waste_rules.csv`: which waste classes each facility may process and
  their handling costs.
- `facility_receiving_windows.csv`: facility dock windows, unload event limits,
  and kg limits by window.
- `incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv`: clinic pairs that cannot be co-loaded before
  an unload.
- `chargers.csv`: charger node, rate, expected queue delay, daily energy cap,
  session limit, and energy price.
- `travel_energy_arcs.csv`: directed travel time, distance, empty-trip energy,
  population-risk index, and loaded-curfew flag between nodes.

The hidden evaluator is not available in the public agent environment. Rebuild
your own feasibility checks from the public data and record them in
`submissions/solve_log.md`.

## Required Output

Create these artifacts under `submissions/`:

- `model.md`: your interpretation of the planning problem and the method you used.
- `solve.py`: a script that creates the submitted route plan from the public data.
- `solution.csv`: the route-event table described below.
- `solve_log.md`: commands run, solver or heuristic status, objective estimate,
  and feasibility checks.

The `solution.csv` file must have exactly these columns:

```csv
row_type,vehicle_id,sequence,node_id,clinic_id,facility_id,charger_id,charge_kwh
```

For `start` and `end` rows, fill `vehicle_id`, `sequence`, and `node_id`; leave
`clinic_id`, `facility_id`, `charger_id`, and `charge_kwh` blank.

For `pickup` rows, fill the clinic and its node. Leave facility, charger, and
charge fields blank.

For `unload` rows, fill the facility and its node. Leave clinic, charger, and
charge fields blank.

For `charge` rows, fill the charger, its node, and a positive `charge_kwh`.
Leave clinic and facility blank.

result28. Command output
---
name: advanced-mip-patterns
description: Use when a task involves fixed charges, logical implications, min/max choices, sequencing, no-overlap, piecewise costs, routing-state logic, or other advanced MIP modeling patterns.
metadata:
  short-description: Advanced MIP formulation patterns
---

# Advanced MIP Patterns

## Use when

- The formulation needs logical implications, fixed charges, activation decisions, ordering, no-overlap, disjunctions, min/max, absolute values, tiers, route state, or resource calendars.
- A simple LP/MIP is producing unrealistic behavior because decisions are not linked tightly enough.
- The model is slow or infeasible and the likely cause is weak big-M logic, excessive pairwise variables, or missing state transitions.

## Do not use when

- A continuous LP is sufficient and adding binaries would not represent a real decision.
- The task is better handled by a specialized CP-SAT, constraint programming, nonlinear, or simulation method and the user did not require MIP.
- The user only needs a high-level explanation; do not introduce advanced constructs unless they clarify the actual model.

## General boundary rules

- Add binaries only for real yes/no choices, disjunctions, or nonconvex structure.
- Prefer the tightest formulation that matches the business rule; avoid decorative binaries.
- Use solver-native constructs when they improve clarity and are supported by the target environment.
- If a construct makes the model much slower, compare it with a simpler valid alternative before keeping it.

## Fixed charge and activation

- Use a binary `y` for whether an activity/resource is active.
- Link activity with a tight bound: `x <= U * y`.
- Add lower activation only when the business requires minimum use: `x >= L * y`.
- Charge fixed cost once through `fixed_cost * y`, not per unit.
- Do not force `y = 1` whenever `x = 0` unless the activity is mandatory.
- If there are startup/shutdown costs over time, separate active state from transition state.

## Logical rules

- Prefer solver-native indicator constraints for simple if-then rules when they keep the model clearer.
- If using big-M, derive M from data bounds and keep one M per rule family when possible.
- For either-or choices, use binaries whose sum is exactly one or at most one, matching the business rule.
- Distinguish implication (`A -> B`) from equivalence (`A <-> B`); the reverse direction often needs a separate constraint.
- For conditional capacity, link both activity and resource use to the condition.

## Min, max, absolute value

- For `z = max(a, b)`, add `z >= a`, `z >= b`, and objective pressure if minimizing z. Use binaries only when equality is needed without objective pressure.
- For absolute deviation `d = |x - target|`, add `d >= x - target` and `d >= target - x`.
- For service-level penalties, separate hard feasibility from soft penalty variables.
- For `min`, either transform with signs or add binaries when equality is not enforced by the objective.
- For ratio constraints, cross-multiply only when denominators are constant or safely bounded away from zero.

## Sequencing and no-overlap

- For jobs sharing a resource, use order binary `before[i,j]`.
- Typical pattern:
  - `start[j] >= end[i] - M * (1 - before[i,j])`
  - `start[i] >= end[j] - M * before[i,j]`
- Compute M from the scheduling horizon, not from an arbitrary large number.
- Include setup, cleanup, travel, rest, or turnover time in the correct side of the ordering constraint.
- Create pairwise order variables only for pairs that can share a resource or conflict.
- If time is discrete and the horizon is small, a time-indexed model may be cleaner than pairwise disjunctions.

## Piecewise and tiers

- Use solver-native piecewise-linear features for convex piecewise costs when suitable.
- For nonconvex tiers, use explicit segment variables and segment activation binaries.
- Ensure tier quantities sum to the original activity and each tier has its own upper bound.
- Distinguish incremental tiers from all-units tiers; their formulations and costs differ.
- Check whether discount tiers create nonconvex incentives that require binary selection.

## State and inventory logic

- State variables need initial conditions, transition equations, and terminal requirements if the business specifies them.
- Route or schedule state should be advanced in event order, not checked only row by row.
- Recovery, restock, cleaning, cooldown, maintenance, and rest rules often require cumulative state or last-event logic.
- If state can reset, model the reset trigger and its resource/time cost.

## Debugging advanced models

- First solve a relaxed or smaller instance and inspect whether the intended binaries activate.
- Add named constraints so IIS output points to business rules.
- If the reference solution is infeasible, replay the relevant state transitions manually before changing the evaluator.
- If the LP relaxation is very weak, inspect big-M values, missing lower/upper links, and symmetry.
- If the model is unexpectedly infeasible, test each advanced rule family on a tiny instance with a known feasible solution.

/bin/bash -lc "python - <<'PY'
import csv, itertools, math, json
from collections import defaultdict
base='/app/data'
# load data
with open(base+'/config.json') as f: config=json.load(f)
with open(base+'/clinics.csv') as f: clinics={r['clinic_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/vehicles.csv') as f: vehicles={r['vehicle_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/treatment_facilities.csv') as f: facilities={r['facility_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/facility_waste_rules.csv') as f: fw={(r['facility_id'],r['waste_type']):int(r['accepted']) for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/travel_energy_arcs.csv') as f: arcs={(r['from_node'],r['to_node']):r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
inc=set()
with open(base+'/incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv') as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f):
        inc.add(tuple(sorted((r['clinic_a'],r['clinic_b']))))
zone_map={
'west':('F1','EV5','EV7'),
'south':('F3','EV3','EV6'),
'east':('F2','EV2'),
'southeast':('F2','EV4'),
'southwest':('F3','EV1'),
'north':('F5','EV8'),
'central':('F5','EV8'),
}
zone_clinics=defaultdict(list)
for c in clinics.values(): zone_clinics[c['zone']].append(c['clinic_id'])
curfew=(config['curfew_start_min'],config['curfew_end_min'])
load_coeff=config['load_energy_kwh_per_kg_km']

def arc_cost(from_node,to_node,loadkg):
    a=arcs[(from_node,to_node)]
    travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
    wait=0.0
    if loadkg>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1:
        # if traversal would intersect curfew, wait until end
        if depart_time < curfew[1] and depart_time + travel > curfew[0]:
            wait=max(0.0, curfew[1]-depart_time)
    energy=empty + load_coeff*loadkg*dist
    return travel,dist,energy,wait

def simulate(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery=None):
    v=vehicles[vehicle_id]; fac_node=facilities[facility_id]['node_id']
    total_cap=float(v['capacity_kg']); red_cap=float(v['red_compartment_kg']); y_cap=float(v['yellow_compartment_kg'])
    battery=float(v['start_battery_kwh']) if start_battery is None else start_battery
    time=start_time; node=start_node
    load_r=load_y=0.0; onboard=[]; obj=0.0
    for cid in order:
        c=clinics[cid]
        a=arcs[(node,c['node_id'])]
        travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
        wait_curf=0.0
        if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time + travel > curfew[0]:
            wait_curf=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
        energy=empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist
        time += wait_curf + travel
        battery -= energy
        node=c['node_id']
        start=max(time,float(c['earliest_min']))
        if start > float(c['latest_min'])+1e-9:
            return None
        time=start+float(c['service_min'])
        # incompatibility when adding new clinic to onboard set
        for oc in onboard:
            if tuple(sorted((cid,oc[0]))) in inc:
                return None
        load_r += float(c['red_kg']); load_y += float(c['yellow_kg'])
        if load_r+load_y > total_cap+1e-9 or load_r>red_cap+1e-9 or load_y>y_cap+1e-9:
            return None
        onboard.append((cid,time))
        if battery < -1e-6:
            return None
    # to facility
    a=arcs[(node,fac_node)]
    travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
    wait_curf=0.0
    if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time + travel > curfew[0]:
        wait_curf=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
    energy=empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist
    time += wait_curf + travel
    battery -= energy
    if battery < -1e-6:
        return None
    unload_time=time
    # onboard time check
    for cid,pick_time in onboard:
        c=clinics[cid]
        if unload_time - pick_time > float(c['max_red_onboard_min'])+1e-9:
            return None
        if unload_time - pick_time > float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-9:
            return None
    return {'end_time':time,'end_node':fac_node,'battery':battery,'order':order,'kg':load_r+load_y,'red':load_r,'yellow':load_y}

def best_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,subset,facility_id,start_battery=None):
    best=None
    for order in itertools.permutations(subset):
        res=simulate(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery)
        if res is None: continue
        score=res['end_time']
        if best is None or score < best['end_time']:
            best=res
    return best

# test north single route and central route
print('north size',len(zone_clinics['north']))
res=best_trip('EV8','D0',360,zone_clinics['north'],'F5')
print('north best',res and {k:res[k] for k in ('end_time','battery','kg','red')}, res and res['order'])
res2=best_trip('EV8','D0',360,zone_clinics['central'],'F5')
print('central best',res2 and {k:res2[k] for k in ('end_time','battery','kg','red')}, res2 and res2['order'])
PY"

/bin/bash -lc "python - <<'PY'
import csv, itertools, json
from collections import defaultdict
base='/app/data'
with open(base+'/config.json') as f: config=json.load(f)
with open(base+'/clinics.csv') as f: clinics={r['clinic_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/vehicles.csv') as f: vehicles={r['vehicle_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/treatment_facilities.csv') as f: facilities={r['facility_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/travel_energy_arcs.csv') as f: arcs={(r['from_node'],r['to_node']):r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
inc=set();
with open(base+'/incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv') as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f): inc.add(tuple(sorted((r['clinic_a'],r['clinic_b']))))
curfew=(config['curfew_start_min'],config['curfew_end_min']); load_coeff=config['load_energy_kwh_per_kg_km']

def simulate(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery):
    v=vehicles[vehicle_id]; fac_node=facilities[facility_id]['node_id']
    total_cap=float(v['capacity_kg']); red_cap=float(v['red_compartment_kg']); y_cap=float(v['yellow_compartment_kg'])
    battery=start_battery; time=start_time; node=start_node; load_r=load_y=0.0; onboard=[]
    for cid in order:
        c=clinics[cid]; a=arcs[(node,c['node_id'])]
        travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
        wait=0.0
        if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time+travel > curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
        battery -= empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist; time += wait+travel; node=c['node_id']
        start=max(time,float(c['earliest_min']))
        if start>float(c['latest_min'])+1e-9 or battery < -1e-6: return None
        time=start+float(c['service_min'])
        for oc,_ in onboard:
            if tuple(sorted((cid,oc))) in inc: return None
        load_r += float(c['red_kg']); load_y += float(c['yellow_kg'])
        if load_r+load_y > total_cap+1e-9 or load_r>red_cap+1e-9 or load_y>y_cap+1e-9: return None
        onboard.append((cid,time))
    a=arcs[(node,fac_node)]; travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
    wait=0.0
    if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time+travel > curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
    battery -= empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist; time += wait+travel
    if battery < -1e-6: return None
    for cid,pick in onboard:
        c=clinics[cid]
        if time-pick > float(c['max_red_onboard_min'])+1e-9 or time-pick > float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-9: return None
    return {'end_time':time,'battery':battery,'node':fac_node,'order':order}

def best(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,subset,facility_id,start_battery):
    best=None
    for order in itertools.permutations(subset):
        res=simulate(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery)
        if res and (best is None or res['end_time']<best['end_time']): best=res
    return best
north=['H05','H11','H17','H23','H29','H35','H39']
central=['H43','H44']
first=best('EV8','D0',360,north,'F5',156.0)
print('north first',first)
second=best('EV8','TF5',first['end_time'],central,'F5',first['battery'])
print('then central',second)
first2=best('EV8','D0',360,central,'F5',156.0)
print('central first',first2)
second2=best('EV8','TF5',first2['end_time'],north,'F5',first2['battery'])
print('then north',second2)

/bin/bash -lc "python - <<'PY'
import csv, itertools, json, math
from collections import defaultdict
base='/app/data'
with open(base+'/config.json') as f: config=json.load(f)
with open(base+'/clinics.csv') as f: clinics={r['clinic_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/vehicles.csv') as f: vehicles={r['vehicle_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/treatment_facilities.csv') as f: facilities={r['facility_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/travel_energy_arcs.csv') as f: arcs={(r['from_node'],r['to_node']):r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
inc=set();
with open(base+'/incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv') as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f): inc.add(tuple(sorted((r['clinic_a'],r['clinic_b']))))
curfew=(config['curfew_start_min'],config['curfew_end_min']); load_coeff=config['load_energy_kwh_per_kg_km']
zone_clinics=defaultdict(list)
with open(base+'/clinics.csv') as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f): zone_clinics[r['zone']].append(r['clinic_id'])

def simulate_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery):
    v=vehicles[vehicle_id]; fac_node=facilities[facility_id]['node_id']
    total_cap=float(v['capacity_kg']); red_cap=float(v['red_compartment_kg']); y_cap=float(v['yellow_compartment_kg'])
    battery=start_battery; time=start_time; node=start_node; load_r=load_y=0.0; onboard=[]
    distsum=0.0
    for cid in order:
        c=clinics[cid]; a=arcs[(node,c['node_id'])]
        travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
        wait=0.0
        if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time+travel > curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
        battery -= empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist; time += wait+travel; distsum += dist; node=c['node_id']
        start=max(time,float(c['earliest_min']))
        if start>float(c['latest_min'])+1e-9 or battery < -1e-6: return None
        time=start+float(c['service_min'])
        for oc,_ in onboard:
            if tuple(sorted((cid,oc))) in inc: return None
        load_r += float(c['red_kg']); load_y += float(c['yellow_kg'])
        if load_r+load_y > total_cap+1e-9 or load_r>red_cap+1e-9 or load_y>y_cap+1e-9: return None
        onboard.append((cid,time))
    a=arcs[(node,fac_node)]; travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
    wait=0.0
    if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time+travel > curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
    battery -= empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist; time += wait+travel; distsum += dist
    if battery < -1e-6: return None
    for cid,pick in onboard:
        c=clinics[cid]
        if time-pick > float(c['max_red_onboard_min'])+1e-9 or time-pick > float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-9: return None
    return {'end_time':time,'battery':battery,'node':fac_node,'order':order,'dist':distsum,'kg':load_r+load_y,'red':load_r,'yellow':load_y}

def end_depot(vehicle_id,node,time,battery):
    v=vehicles[vehicle_id]; end=v['end_node']
    a=arcs[(node,end)]
    battery2=battery-float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
    time2=time+float(a['travel_min'])
    if battery2 < -1e-6 or time2 > float(v['shift_end_min'])+1e-9: return None
    return {'end_time':time2,'battery':battery2}

def best_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,subset,facility_id,start_battery):
    best=None
    for order in itertools.permutations(subset):
        res=simulate_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery)
        if res is None: continue
        end=end_depot(vehicle_id,res['node'],res['end_time'],res['battery'])
        if end is None: continue
        score=end['end_time']
        if best is None or score<best['score']:
            best={**res,'final_end_time':end['end_time'],'final_battery':end['battery'],'score':score}
    return best

def best_two_trips(vehicle_id,zone,facility_id):
    ids=zone_clinics[zone]
    n=len(ids)
    best=None
    allmask=(1<<n)-1
    for mask in range(1,allmask):
        if not (mask & 1):
            continue
        A=[ids[i] for i in range(n) if mask>>i &1]
        B=[ids[i] for i in range(n) if not (mask>>i &1)]
        if not B: continue
        for first,second in [(A,B),(B,A)]:
            r1=best_trip(vehicle_id,'D0',float(vehicles[vehicle_id]['shift_start_min']),first,facility_id,float(vehicles[vehicle_id]['start_battery_kwh']))
            if r1 is None: continue
            r2=best_trip(vehicle_id,facilities[facility_id]['node_id'],r1['end_time'],second,facility_id,r1['battery'])
            if r2 is None: continue
            final=end_depot(vehicle_id,r2['node'],r2['end_time'],r2['battery'])
            if final is None: continue
            score=final['end_time']
            if best is None or score<best['score']:
                best={'score':score,'first':r1,'second':r2,'final':final,'partition':(tuple(first),tuple(second))}
    return best

def best_partition_two_vehicles(zone,facility_id,veh1,veh2):
    ids=zone_clinics[zone]; n=len(ids); allmask=(1<<n)-1; best=None
    for mask in range(1,allmask):
        if not (mask & 1): continue
        A=[ids[i] for i in range(n) if mask>>i &1]
        B=[ids[i] for i in range(n) if not(mask>>i &1)]
        if not B: continue
        for part in [((veh1,A),(veh2,B)),((veh1,B),(veh2,A))]:
            feasible=[]; score=0.0
            ok=True
            for veh,subset in part:
                r=best_trip(veh,'D0',float(vehicles[veh]['shift_start_min']),subset,facility_id,float(vehicles[veh]['start_battery_kwh']))
                if r is None:
                    ok=False; break
                feasible.append((veh,r,subset)); score=max(score,r['final_end_time'])
            if ok and (best is None or score<best['score']):
                best={'score':score,'routes':feasible}
    return best

cases=[('west','F1','EV5','EV7'),('south','F3','EV3','EV6')]
for z,f,v1,v2 in cases:
    b=best_partition_two_vehicles(z,f,v1,v2)
    print('\\nZONE',z,b['score'])
    for veh,r,subset in b['routes']:
        print(veh,subset,r['order'],round(r['end_time'],1),round(r['final_end_time'],1),round(r['final_battery'],1),round(r['kg'],1),round(r['red'],1))
for z,f,v in [('east','F2','EV2'),('southeast','F2','EV4'),('southwest','F3','EV1')]:
    b=best_two_trips(v,z,f)
    print('\\nZONE',z,b['score'])
    for name in ['first','second']:
        r=b[name]
        print(name,r['order'],round(r['end_time'],1),round(r['battery'],1),round(r['kg'],1),round(r['red'],1))
    print('final',round(b['final']['end_time'],1),round(b['final']['battery'],1))
# north + central two trips both orders exhaustive
from itertools import permutations
north=zone_clinics['north']; central=zone_clinics['central']
bn=best_trip('EV8','D0',360,north,'F5',float(vehicles['EV8']['start_battery_kwh']))
bc=best_trip('EV8','D0',360,central,'F5',float(vehicles['EV8']['start_battery_kwh']))
print('\\nNORTH',bn['order'],bn['final_end_time'],bn['final_battery'])
print('CENTRAL',bc['order'],bc['final_end_time'],bc['final_battery'])

obj=0.0
for vid in plan:
    v=vehicles[vid]; p=permits[vid]
    seqrows=[r for r in rows if r[1]==vid]
    time=float(v['shift_start_min']); battery=float(v['start_battery_kwh']); node=v['start_node']; loadr=loady=0.0; onboard=[]; iso=0; used=False
    for idx,r in enumerate(seqrows[1:],start=1):
        rtype,_,seq,node_id,clinic_id,facility_id,charger_id,charge_kwh=r
        a=arcs[(node,node_id)]; travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh']); pop=float(a['population_risk_index'])
        wait=0.0
        if loadr+loady>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time+travel > curfew[0]:
            wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
        weighted=red_factor*loadr + yellow_factor*loady
        obj += travel_cost_km*dist
        obj += risk_cost * weighted * pop * travel
        obj += risk_cost * weighted * wait_mult * wait
        energy=empty + load_coeff*(loadr+loady)*dist
        battery -= energy
        time += wait + travel
        node=node_id
        if battery < -1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} battery negative before seq {seq}')
        if rtype=='pickup':
            used=True
            c=clinics[clinic_id]
            if clinic_id in picked: violations.append(f'duplicate pickup {clinic_id}')
            picked.add(clinic_id)
            start=max(time,float(c['earliest_min']))
            if start > float(c['latest_min'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} late service {clinic_id}')
            time=start+float(c['service_min'])
            pr=c['priority_class']
            if int(p['allow_'+pr])"'!=1: violations.append(f'"'{vid} lacks permit {clinic_id}')
            if pr=='isolation':
                iso += 1
                if iso > int(p['max_isolation_pickups']): violations.append(f'{vid} iso cap')
            for oc,_ in onboard:
                if tuple(sorted((clinic_id,oc))) in inc: violations.append(f'{vid} incompatible {clinic_id} with {oc}')
            loadr += float(c['red_kg']); loady += float(c['yellow_kg'])
            if loadr+loady > float(v['capacity_kg'])+1e-6 or loadr > float(v['red_compartment_kg'])+1e-6 or loady > float(v['yellow_compartment_kg'])+1e-6:
                violations.append(f'{vid} capacity exceeded')
            onboard.append((clinic_id,time))
        elif rtype=='unload':
            used=True
            if loadr+loady <= 1e-9: violations.append(f'{vid} empty unload {facility_id}')
            fac=facilities[facility_id]
            if time > float(fac['latest_receive_min'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} unload after facility latest {facility_id}')
            found=None
            for w in windows:
                if w['facility_id']"'!=facility_id: continue
                s=float(w['"'start_min']); e=float(w['end_min'])
                if time < s: time=s
                if s <= time <= e+1e-9:
                    found=w; break
            if found is None: violations.append(f'{vid} no receiving window {facility_id}')
            if facility_accept[facility_id].get('red',0)"'!=1 and loadr>1e-9: violations.append(f'"'{facility_id} rejects red')
            if facility_accept[facility_id].get('yellow',0)"'!=1 and loady>1e-9: violations.append(f'"'{facility_id} rejects yellow')
            for cid,pick_t in onboard:
                c=clinics[cid]
                if time-pick_t > float(c['max_red_onboard_min'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} red onboard exceeded {cid}')
                if time-pick_t > float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} yellow onboard exceeded {cid}')
            facility_tot[facility_id]+=loadr+loady; facility_red[facility_id]+=loadr; facility_yel[facility_id]+=loady; opened.add(facility_id)
            key=(facility_id,found['window_id'])
            window_use[key][0]+=1; window_use[key][1]+=loadr+loady
            obj += treat_cost[facility_id]['red']*loadr + treat_cost[facility_id]['yellow']*loady
            loadr=loady=0.0; onboard=[]
        elif rtype=='charge':
            ch=chargers[charger_id]; q=float(ch['queue_delay_min']); amt=float(charge_kwh)
            if amt <= 0: violations.append(f'{vid} nonpositive charge')
            time += q + amt/float(ch['rate_kwh_per_min'])
            battery += amt
            if battery > float(v['battery_kwh'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} battery over cap')
            station_use[charger_id][0]+=1; station_use[charger_id][1]+=amt
            obj += amt*float(ch['energy_price'])
        elif rtype=='end':
            if loadr+loady > 1e-9: violations.append(f'{vid} ends loaded')
    if used: obj += float(v['fixed_cost'])
    if node "'!= v['"'end_node']: violations.append(f'{vid} wrong end node')
    if time > float(v['shift_end_min'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} shift exceeded')
for fid in opened: obj += float(facilities[fid]['open_cost'])
if len(picked)"'!=len(clinics): violations.append(f'"'missing pickups {len(clinics)-len(picked)}')
if len(opened)>config['max_open_facilities']: violations.append('too many open facilities')
for fid in opened:
    if facility_tot[fid] + 1e-6 < config['minimum_facility_throughput_kg']: violations.append(f'{fid} min throughput')
    fac=facilities[fid]
    if facility_tot[fid] > float(fac['daily_capacity_kg'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{fid} daily cap')
    if facility_red[fid] > float(fac['red_capacity_kg'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{fid} red cap')
    if facility_yel[fid] > float(fac['yellow_capacity_kg'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{fid} yellow cap')
for w in windows:
    key=(w['facility_id'],w['window_id'])
    if window_use[key][0] > int(w['max_unloads']): violations.append(f'window unload cap {key}')
    if window_use[key][1] > float(w['max_kg'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'window kg cap {key}')
for cid,ch in chargers.items():
    if station_use[cid][0] > int(ch['session_limit']): violations.append(f'charger session {cid}')
    if station_use[cid][1] > float(ch['daily_kwh_cap'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'charger kwh {cid}')
print('violations',violations)
print('opened',opened)
print('facility totals',dict(facility_tot))
print('window use',dict(window_use))
print('objective_est',round(obj,4))

def simulate_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery):
    v=vehicles[vehicle_id]; fac_node=facilities[facility_id]['node_id']; p=permits[vehicle_id]
    total_cap=float(v['capacity_kg']); red_cap=float(v['red_compartment_kg']); y_cap=float(v['yellow_compartment_kg'])
    battery=start_battery; time=start_time; node=start_node; load_r=load_y=0.0; onboard=[]; iso=0.0
    for cid in order:
        c=clinics[cid]; a=arcs[(node,c['node_id'])]
        travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
        wait=0.0
        if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time+travel > curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
        battery -= empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist; time += wait+travel; node=c['node_id']
        start=max(time,float(c['earliest_min']))
        if start>float(c['latest_min'])+1e-9 or battery < -1e-6: return None
        time=start+float(c['service_min'])
        if c['priority_class']=='isolation':
            iso += 1
            if iso > int(p['max_isolation_pickups']): return None
        for oc,_ in onboard:
            if tuple(sorted((cid,oc))) in inc: return None
        load_r += float(c['red_kg']); load_y += float(c['yellow_kg'])
        if load_r+load_y > total_cap+1e-9 or load_r>red_cap+1e-9 or load_y>y_cap+1e-9: return None
        onboard.append((cid,time))
    a=arcs[(node,fac_node)]; travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh'])
    wait=0.0
    if load_r+load_y>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time < curfew[1] and time+travel > curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
    battery -= empty + load_coeff*(load_r+load_y)*dist; time += wait+travel
    if battery < -1e-6: return None
    for cid,pick in onboard:
        c=clinics[cid]
        if time-pick > float(c['max_red_onboard_min'])+1e-9 or time-pick > float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-9: return None
    return {'end_time':time,'battery':battery,'node':fac_node,'order':order,'kg':load_r+load_y,'red':load_r}

def best_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,subset,facility_id,start_battery):
    best=None
    for order in itertools.permutations(subset):
        r=simulate_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,order,facility_id,start_battery)
        if r and (best is None or r['end_time']<best['end_time']): best=r
    return best

import csv, itertools, json, math
from collections import defaultdict
base='/app/data'
with open(base+'/config.json') as f: config=json.load(f)
with open(base+'/clinics.csv') as f: clinics={r['clinic_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/vehicles.csv') as f: vehicles={r['vehicle_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/vehicle_waste_permits.csv') as f: permits={r['vehicle_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/treatment_facilities.csv') as f: facilities={r['facility_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
with open(base+'/facility_receiving_windows.csv') as f: windows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
with open(base+'/travel_energy_arcs.csv') as f: arcs={(r['from_node'],r['to_node']):r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
inc=set();
with open(base+'/incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv') as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f): inc.add(tuple(sorted((r['clinic_a'],r['clinic_b']))))
curfew=(config['curfew_start_min'],config['curfew_end_min']); load_coeff=config['load_energy_kwh_per_kg_km']
zone_clinics=defaultdict(list)
with open(base+'/clinics.csv') as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f): zone_clinics[r['zone']].append(r['clinic_id'])

def trip_window(fid,time):
    for w in windows:
        if w['facility_id']"'!=fid: continue
        s=float(w['"'start_min']); e=float(w['end_min'])
        t=max(time,s)
        if t<=e+1e-9: return w['window_id'], t
    return None, None

def sim_trip(vehicle_id,start_node,start_time,subset,facility_id,start_battery):
    v=vehicles[vehicle_id]; p=permits[vehicle_id]; fac_node=facilities[facility_id]['node_id']
    total_cap=float(v['capacity_kg']); red_cap=float(v['red_compartment_kg']); y_cap=float(v['yellow_compartment_kg'])
    best=None
    for order in itertools.permutations(subset):
      battery=start_battery; time=start_time; node=start_node; loadr=loady=0.0; onboard=[]; iso=0; ok=True
      for cid in order:
        c=clinics[cid]
        a=arcs[(node,c['node_id'])]
        travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh']); wait=0.0
        if loadr+loady>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time<curfew[1] and time+travel>curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
        battery -= empty + load_coeff*(loadr+loady)*dist; time += wait+travel; node=c['node_id']
        start=max(time,float(c['earliest_min']))
        if start>float(c['latest_min'])+1e-9 or battery<-1e-6: ok=False; break
        time=start+float(c['service_min'])
        if c['priority_class']=='isolation':
            iso+=1
            if iso>int(p['max_isolation_pickups']): ok=False; break
        for oc,_ in onboard:
            if tuple(sorted((cid,oc))) in inc: ok=False; break
        if not ok: break
        loadr += float(c['red_kg']); loady += float(c['yellow_kg'])
        if loadr+loady>total_cap+1e-9 or loadr>red_cap+1e-9 or loady>y_cap+1e-9: ok=False; break
        onboard.append((cid,time))
      if not ok: continue
      a=arcs[(node,fac_node)]
      travel=float(a['travel_min']); dist=float(a['distance_km']); empty=float(a['empty_energy_kwh']); wait=0.0
      if loadr+loady>1e-9 and int(a['loaded_curfew_flag'])==1 and time<curfew[1] and time+travel>curfew[0]: wait=max(0.0,curfew[1]-time)
      battery -= empty + load_coeff*(loadr+loady)*dist; time += wait+travel
      if battery<-1e-6: continue
      for cid,pick in onboard:
        c=clinics[cid]
        if time-pick>float(c['max_red_onboard_min'])+1e-9 or time-pick>float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-9: ok=False
      if not ok: continue
      wid,adj_time=trip_window(facility_id,time)
      if wid is None or adj_time>float(facilities[facility_id]['latest_receive_min'])+1e-9: continue
      if best is None or adj_time<best['end_time']:
        best={'order':order,'end_time':adj_time,'battery':battery,'node':fac_node,'facility':facility_id,'window':wid,'kg':round(loadr+loady,10),'red':round(loadr,10),'yellow':round(loady,10)}
    return best

def gen_two_trip_patterns(vehicle_id,zone,fac_choices,top=200):
    ids=zone_clinics[zone]; n=len(ids); allmask=(1<<n)-1; out=[]
    for mask in range(1,allmask):
        if not(mask&1): continue
        A=[ids[i] for i in range(n) if mask>>i&1]; B=[ids[i] for i in range(n) if not(mask>>i&1)]
        if not B: continue
        for first,second in [(A,B),(B,A)]:
            for f1 in fac_choices:
                r1=sim_trip(vehicle_id,'D0',float(vehicles[vehicle_id]['shift_start_min']),first,f1,float(vehicles[vehicle_id]['start_battery_kwh']))
                if not r1: continue
                for f2 in fac_choices:
                    r2=sim_trip(vehicle_id,facilities[f1]['node_id'],r1['end_time'],second,f2,r1['battery'])
                    if not r2: continue
                    endarc=arcs[(facilities[f2]['node_id'],vehicles[vehicle_id]['end_node'])]
                    final_time=r2['end_time']+float(endarc['travel_min'])
                    final_bat=r2['battery']-float(endarc['empty_energy_kwh'])
                    if final_bat<-1e-6 or final_time>float(vehicles[vehicle_id]['shift_end_min'])+1e-9: continue
                    out.append({'vehicle':vehicle_id,'trips':[r1,r2],'final_time':final_time,'final_bat':final_bat})
    # dedupe by route signature
    uniq={}
    for p in out:
        sig=tuple((tuple(t['order']),t['facility']) for t in p['trips'])
        if sig not in uniq or p['final_time']<uniq[sig]['final_time']:
            uniq[sig]=p
    out=list(uniq.values())
    out.sort(key=lambda p:p['final_time'])
    return out[:top]

def gen_north_central_patterns(top=100):
    out=[]
    north=zone_clinics['north']; central=zone_clinics['central']
    tasks=[('north',north,['F5']),('central',central,['F3','F5','F1'])]
    for seq in [(tasks[0],tasks[1]),(tasks[1],tasks[0])]:
        for f1 in seq[0][2]:
            r1=sim_trip('EV8','D0',360,seq[0][1],f1,float(vehicles['EV8']['start_battery_kwh']))
            if not r1: continue
            for f2 in seq[1][2]:
                r2=sim_trip('EV8',facilities[f1]['node_id'],r1['end_time'],seq[1][1],f2,r1['battery'])
                if not r2: continue
                endarc=arcs[(facilities[f2]['node_id'],'D0')]
                final_time=r2['end_time']+float(endarc['travel_min'])
                final_bat=r2['battery']-float(endarc['empty_energy_kwh'])
                if final_bat<-1e-6 or final_time>float(vehicles['EV8']['shift_end_min'])+1e-9: continue
                out.append({'vehicle':'EV8','trips':[r1,r2],'final_time':final_time,'final_bat':final_bat,'seq':(seq[0][0],seq[1][0])})
    uniq={}
    for p in out:
        sig=(p['seq'],tuple((tuple(t['order']),t['facility']) for t in p['trips']))
        if sig not in uniq or p['final_time']<uniq[sig]['final_time']:
            uniq[sig]=p
    out=list(uniq.values())
    out.sort(key=lambda p:p['final_time'])
    return out[:top]
# Fixed base trips
base=[]
for veh,subset,fac in [('EV5',['H01','H19','H07'],'F1'),('EV7',['H37','H31','H13','H25'],'F1'),('EV3',['H06','H12','H18'],'F3'),('EV6',['H30','H40','H24','H36'],'F3')]:

    # quick daily check partial
    loads=defaultdict(lambda:[0,0.0,0.0,0.0])
    win=defaultdict(lambda:[0,0.0])
    opened=set()
    for p in base+[p1,p2]:
      for t in p['trips']:
        L=loads[t['facility']]; L[0]+=1; L[1]+=t['kg']; L[2]+=t['red']; L[3]+=t['yellow']; opened.add(t['facility'])
        W=win[(t['facility'],t['window'])]; W[0]+=1; W[1]+=t['kg']
    for fid,v in loads.items():
      if v[1]>fac_daily[fid]+1e-6 or v[2]>fac_redcap[fid]+1e-6 or v[3]>fac_ycap[fid]+1e-6: ok=False; break
    for k,v in win.items():
      cap=window_caps[k]
      if v[0]>cap[0] or v[1]>cap[1]+1e-6: ok=False; break
    if not ok: continue
    for p4 in ev4:

      ok=True

        W=win2.setdefault((t['facility'],t['window']),[0,0.0]); W[0]+=1; W[1]+=t['kg']

          if v[0]>cap[0] or v[1]>cap[1]+1e-6: ok=False; break

        win3={k:v[:] for k,v in win2.items()}

        ok=True

            cap=window_caps[k]
            if v[0]>cap[0] or v[1]>cap[1]+1e-6: ok=False; break
        if not ok: continue
        score=max(p['final_time'] for p in base+[p1,p2,p4,p8])
        if best is None or score<best[0]:

          print('new best',score)
          print('ev1',[(t['order'],t['facility'],t['window'],t['kg']) for t in p1['trips']])
          print('ev2',[(t['order'],t['facility'],t['window'],t['kg']) for t in p2['trips']])
          print('ev4',[(t['order'],t['facility'],t['window'],t['kg']) for t in p4['trips']])
          print('ev8',p8['seq'],[(t['order'],t['facility'],t['window'],t['kg']) for t in p8['trips']])
          print('loads', {k:v[1] for k,v in loads3.items()})
          print('wins', {k:v for k,v in win3.items()})
          raise SystemExit

            found=None
            for w in windows:
                if w['facility_id']"'!=facility_id: continue
                s=float(w['"'start_min']); e=float(w['end_min'])
                if time < s:
                    time=s
                if s <= time <= e+1e-9:
                    found=w; break
            if found is None: violations.append(f'{vid} no receiving window {facility_id}')
            if facility_accept[facility_id].get('red',0)"'!=1 and loadr>1e-9: violations.append(f'"'{facility_id} rejects red')
            if facility_accept[facility_id].get('yellow',0)"'!=1 and loady>1e-9: violations.append(f'"'{facility_id} rejects yellow')
            for cid,pick_t in onboard:

                if time-pick_t > float(c['max_yellow_onboard_min'])+1e-6: violations.append(f'{vid} yellow onboard exceeded {cid}')
            facility_tot[facility_id]+=loadr+loady; facility_red[facility_id]+=loadr; facility_yel[facility_id]+=loady; opened.add(facility_id)
