from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import itertools
import json
import math
import os
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum


ROOT = Path("/app")
DATA_DIR = ROOT / "data"
SUBMISSIONS_DIR = ROOT / "submissions"

SOLUTION_PATH = SUBMISSIONS_DIR / "solution.csv"
LOG_PATH = SUBMISSIONS_DIR / "solve_log.md"

MASTER_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS = 300.0
REL_GAP = 0.0005
FLOAT_TOL = 1e-8


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TripPlan:
    vehicle_id: str
    clinic_order: Tuple[str, ...]
    facility_id: str
    unload_window_id: str
    unload_time: float
    end_time: float
    end_battery: float
    kg: float
    red_kg: float
    yellow_kg: float
    objective: float


@dataclass
class Pattern:
    group_id: str
    pattern_id: str
    vehicle_sequences: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, str]]]
    objective: float
    final_times: Dict[str, float]
    final_batteries: Dict[str, float]
    facility_total_kg: Dict[str, float]
    facility_red_kg: Dict[str, float]
    facility_yellow_kg: Dict[str, float]
    facility_window_counts: Dict[Tuple[str, str], int]
    facility_window_kg: Dict[Tuple[str, str], float]
    facilities_used: Tuple[str, ...]


def read_csv(path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="") as fh:
        return list(csv.DictReader(fh))


def load_data() -> Dict[str, object]:
    config = json.loads((DATA_DIR / "config.json").read_text())

    clinics = {row["clinic_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "clinics.csv")}
    vehicles = {row["vehicle_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "vehicles.csv")}
    permits = {row["vehicle_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "vehicle_waste_permits.csv")}
    facilities = {row["facility_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "treatment_facilities.csv")}
    chargers = {row["charger_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "chargers.csv")}
    nodes = {row["node_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "nodes.csv")}
    windows = read_csv(DATA_DIR / "facility_receiving_windows.csv")
    travel = {(row["from_node"], row["to_node"]): row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "travel_energy_arcs.csv")}

    facility_rules: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]] = defaultdict(dict)
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "facility_waste_rules.csv"):
        facility_rules[row["facility_id"]][row["waste_type"]] = {
            "accepted": float(row["accepted"]),
            "treatment_cost_per_kg": float(row["treatment_cost_per_kg"]),
        }

    incompatible_pairs = set()
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "incompatible_pickup_pairs.csv"):
        incompatible_pairs.add(tuple(sorted((row["clinic_a"], row["clinic_b"]))))

    zone_clinics: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    for clinic_id, clinic in clinics.items():
        zone_clinics[clinic["zone"]].append(clinic_id)

    windows_by_facility: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, str]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for row in windows:
        windows_by_facility[row["facility_id"]].append(row)
    for rows in windows_by_facility.values():
        rows.sort(key=lambda row: float(row["start_min"]))

    return {
        "config": config,
        "clinics": clinics,
        "vehicles": vehicles,
        "permits": permits,
        "facilities": facilities,
        "chargers": chargers,
        "nodes": nodes,
        "windows": windows,
        "windows_by_facility": windows_by_facility,
        "travel": travel,
        "facility_rules": facility_rules,
        "incompatible_pairs": incompatible_pairs,
        "zone_clinics": zone_clinics,
    }


def as_float(value: str) -> float:
    return float(value)


def format_float(value: float) -> str:
    return f"{value:.8f}"


def weighted_load(red_kg: float, yellow_kg: float, data: Dict[str, object]) -> float:
    config = data["config"]
    return config["red_risk_factor"] * red_kg + config["yellow_risk_factor"] * yellow_kg


def wait_risk_cost(wait_min: float, red_kg: float, yellow_kg: float, data: Dict[str, object]) -> float:
    if wait_min <= FLOAT_TOL:
        return 0.0
    config = data["config"]
    return (
        config["risk_cost_per_unit"]
        * weighted_load(red_kg, yellow_kg, data)
        * config["loaded_wait_risk_multiplier"]
        * wait_min
    )


def advance_arc(
    current_node: str,
    next_node: str,
    departure_time: float,
    battery: float,
    red_kg: float,
    yellow_kg: float,
    data: Dict[str, object],
) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    config = data["config"]
    arc = data["travel"][(current_node, next_node)]

    travel_min = as_float(arc["travel_min"])
    distance_km = as_float(arc["distance_km"])
    empty_energy = as_float(arc["empty_energy_kwh"])
    pop_risk = as_float(arc["population_risk_index"])
    load_kg = red_kg + yellow_kg

    wait_min = 0.0
    if load_kg > FLOAT_TOL and int(arc["loaded_curfew_flag"]) == 1:
        curfew_start = config["curfew_start_min"]
        curfew_end = config["curfew_end_min"]
        if departure_time < curfew_end and departure_time + travel_min > curfew_start:
            wait_min = max(0.0, curfew_end - departure_time)

    route_cost = config["travel_cost_per_km"] * distance_km
    if load_kg > FLOAT_TOL:
        route_cost += (
            config["risk_cost_per_unit"]
            * weighted_load(red_kg, yellow_kg, data)
            * pop_risk
            * travel_min
        )
        route_cost += wait_risk_cost(wait_min, red_kg, yellow_kg, data)

    energy = empty_energy + config["load_energy_kwh_per_kg_km"] * load_kg * distance_km
    return departure_time + wait_min + travel_min, battery - energy, route_cost


class TripSolver:
    def __init__(self, data: Dict[str, object]):
        self.data = data
        self.cache: Dict[Tuple[str, str, float, Tuple[str, ...], str, float], Optional[TripPlan]] = {}

    def best_trip(
        self,
        vehicle_id: str,
        start_node: str,
        start_time: float,
        clinic_subset: Sequence[str],
        facility_id: str,
        start_battery: float,
    ) -> Optional[TripPlan]:
        subset_key = tuple(sorted(clinic_subset))
        cache_key = (
            vehicle_id,
            start_node,
            round(start_time, 8),
            subset_key,
            facility_id,
            round(start_battery, 8),
        )
        if cache_key in self.cache:
            return self.cache[cache_key]

        best: Optional[TripPlan] = None
        for order in itertools.permutations(subset_key):
            trip = self._simulate_trip(vehicle_id, start_node, start_time, order, facility_id, start_battery)
            if trip is None:
                continue
            if best is None or (trip.objective, trip.end_time) < (best.objective, best.end_time):
                best = trip

        self.cache[cache_key] = best
        return best

    def _simulate_trip(
        self,
        vehicle_id: str,
        start_node: str,
        start_time: float,
        clinic_order: Sequence[str],
        facility_id: str,
        start_battery: float,
    ) -> Optional[TripPlan]:
        data = self.data
        clinics = data["clinics"]

                return None
            objective += arc_cost
            node = clinic["node_id"]

            earliest = as_float(clinic["earliest_min"])
            latest = as_float(clinic["latest_min"])
            if time < earliest:
                objective += wait_risk_cost(earliest - time, red_kg, yellow_kg, data)

def generate_north_central_patterns(
    data: Dict[str, object],
    trip_solver: TripSolver,
    limit: int,
) -> List[Pattern]:
    patterns: List[Pattern] = []
    vehicle_id = "EV8"
    pattern_idx = 0

    north_ids = data["zone_clinics"]["north"]
    central_ids = data["zone_clinics"]["central"]
    start_node = data["vehicles"][vehicle_id]["start_node"]
    start_time = as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_id]["shift_start_min"])
    start_battery = as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_id]["start_battery_kwh"])

    tasks = [
        ("north", north_ids, ["F5"]),
        ("central", central_ids, ["F1", "F3", "F5"]),
    ]
    for first_task, second_task in ((tasks[0], tasks[1]), (tasks[1], tasks[0])):
        for facility_1 in first_task[2]:
            trip_1 = trip_solver.best_trip(
                vehicle_id,
                start_node,
                start_time,
                first_task[1],
                facility_1,
                start_battery,
            )
            if trip_1 is None:
                continue
            start_node_2 = data["facilities"][facility_1]["node_id"]
            for facility_2 in second_task[2]:
                trip_2 = trip_solver.best_trip(
                    vehicle_id,
                    start_node_2,
                    trip_1.end_time,
                    second_task[1],
                    facility_2,
                    trip_1.end_battery,
                )
                if trip_2 is None:
                    continue
                pattern = build_pattern(
                    "north_central",
                    f"north_central_{pattern_idx}",
                    data,
                    {vehicle_id: [trip_1, trip_2]},
                )
                if pattern is not None:
                    patterns.append(pattern)
                    pattern_idx += 1
    return keep_top_patterns(patterns, limit)


def keep_top_patterns(patterns: List[Pattern], limit: int) -> List[Pattern]:
    dedup: Dict[Tuple[Tuple[str, Tuple[Tuple[str, str], ...]], ...], Pattern] = {}
    for pattern in patterns:
        signature = pattern_signature(pattern.vehicle_sequences)
        if signature not in dedup or (pattern.objective, max(pattern.final_times.values())) < (
            dedup[signature].objective,
            max(dedup[signature].final_times.values()),
        ):
            dedup[signature] = pattern
    kept = list(dedup.values())
    kept.sort(key=lambda p: (p.objective, max(p.final_times.values())))
    return kept[:limit]

    }
    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        if not patterns:

    return groups


def partial_capacity_feasible(
    selected_patterns: Sequence[Pattern],
    data: Dict[str, object],
) -> bool:
    facilities = data["facilities"]
    window_caps = {
        (row["facility_id"], row["window_id"]): (int(row["max_unloads"]), as_float(row["max_kg"]))
        for row in data["windows"]
    }

    facility_total = defaultdict(float)
    facility_red = defaultdict(float)
    facility_yellow = defaultdict(float)
    window_count = defaultdict(int)
    window_kg = defaultdict(float)

    for pattern in selected_patterns:
        for facility_id, kg in pattern.facility_total_kg.items():
            facility_total[facility_id] += kg
            facility_red[facility_id] += pattern.facility_red_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0)
            facility_yellow[facility_id] += pattern.facility_yellow_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0)
            if facility_total[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["daily_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
            if facility_red[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["red_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
            if facility_yellow[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["yellow_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
        for key, count in pattern.facility_window_counts.items():
            window_count[key] += count
            window_kg[key] += pattern.facility_window_kg[key]
            cap_count, cap_kg = window_caps[key]
            if window_count[key] > cap_count or window_kg[key] > cap_kg + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
    return True


def find_feasible_combination(groups: Dict[str, List[Pattern]], data: Dict[str, object]) -> Dict[str, Pattern]:
    ordered_group_ids = sorted(groups, key=lambda group_id: len(groups[group_id]))
    best_solution: Dict[str, Pattern] = {}

    def backtrack(index: int, chosen: Dict[str, Pattern]) -> bool:
        if index == len(ordered_group_ids):
            best_solution.update(chosen)
            return True
        group_id = ordered_group_ids[index]
        for pattern in groups[group_id]:
            selected = list(chosen.values()) + [pattern]
            if not partial_capacity_feasible(selected, data):
                continue
            chosen[group_id] = pattern
            if backtrack(index + 1, chosen):
                return True
            del chosen[group_id]
        return False

    if not backtrack(0, {}):
        raise RuntimeError("No feasible incumbent found from candidate patterns")
    return best_solution


def solve_master(
    groups: Dict[str, List[Pattern]],
    data: Dict[str, object],
    incumbent: Dict[str, Pattern],
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Pattern], Dict[str, object]]:
    facilities = data["facilities"]
    windows = data["windows"]
    time_limit = MASTER_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS
    env_limit = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
    if env_limit is not None:
        time_limit = min(time_limit, float(env_limit))

    model = Model("medical_waste_pattern_master")
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)

    x_vars = {}
    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
            x_vars[(group_id, idx)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{group_id},{idx}]")

    y_vars = {facility_id: model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"y[{facility_id}]") for facility_id in facilities}

    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[(group_id, idx)] for idx in range(len(patterns))) == 1,
            name=f"choose_one[{group_id}]",
        )

    for facility_id, facility in facilities.items():
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["daily_capacity_kg"]),
            name=f"facility_total[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_red_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["red_capacity_kg"]),
            name=f"facility_red[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_yellow_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["yellow_capacity_kg"]),
            name=f"facility_yellow[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["daily_capacity_kg"]) * y_vars[facility_id],
            name=f"open_link_upper[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            >= data["config"]["minimum_facility_throughput_kg"] * y_vars[facility_id],
            name=f"open_link_lower[{facility_id}]",
        )

    for row in windows:
        key = (row["facility_id"], row["window_id"])
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_window_counts.get(key, 0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= int(row["max_unloads"]),
            name=f"window_count[{key[0]},{key[1]}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_window_kg.get(key, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(row["max_kg"]),
            name=f"window_kg[{key[0]},{key[1]}]",
        )

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(y_vars[facility_id] for facility_id in facilities) <= int(data["config"]["max_open_facilities"]),
        name="max_open_facilities",
    )

    objective = quicksum(
        pattern.objective * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
        for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
    ) + quicksum(
        as_float(facility["open_cost"]) * y_vars[facility_id] for facility_id, facility in facilities.items()
    )
    model.setObjective(objective, "minimize")

    warm = model.createSol()
    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        incumbent_signature = pattern_signature(incumbent[group_id].vehicle_sequences)
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
            value = 1.0 if pattern_signature(pattern.vehicle_sequences) == incumbent_signature else 0.0
            model.setSolVal(warm, x_vars[(group_id, idx)], value)
    used_facilities = set()
    for pattern in incumbent.values():
        used_facilities.update(pattern.facilities_used)
    for facility_id in facilities:
        model.setSolVal(warm, y_vars[facility_id], 1.0 if facility_id in used_facilities else 0.0)
    model.addSol(warm, free=True)

    model.optimize()
    stats = {
        "status": str(model.getStatus()),
        "has_solution": model.getNSols() > 0,
        "best_bound": model.getDualbound() if model.getStage() >= 10 else None,
        "gap": model.getGap() if model.getNSols() > 0 else None,
        "time_limit": time_limit,
    }

    if model.getNSols() == 0:

                time = earliest
            if time > latest + FLOAT_TOL:
                return None

            service_min = as_float(clinic["service_min"])
            objective += wait_risk_cost(service_min, red_kg, yellow_kg, data)
            time += service_min

            start_min = as_float(window["start_min"])
            end_min = as_float(window["end_min"])
            if unload_time < start_min:
                objective += wait_risk_cost(start_min - unload_time, red_kg, yellow_kg, data)
                unload_time = start_min
            if unload_time <= end_min + FLOAT_TOL:
                unload_window_id = window["window_id"]
                matched = True

            return None

        if red_kg > FLOAT_TOL and rules["red"]["accepted"] < 0.5:

            if unload_time - pick_time > as_float(clinic["max_red_onboard_min"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return None
            if unload_time - pick_time > as_float(clinic["max_yellow_onboard_min"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return None

        objective += rules["red"]["treatment_cost_per_kg"] * red_kg
        objective += rules["yellow"]["treatment_cost_per_kg"] * yellow_kg

        return TripPlan(
            vehicle_id=vehicle_id,
            clinic_order=tuple(clinic_order),
            facility_id=facility_id,
            unload_window_id=unload_window_id,
            unload_time=unload_time,
            end_time=unload_time,
            end_battery=battery,
            kg=red_kg + yellow_kg,
            red_kg=red_kg,
            yellow_kg=yellow_kg,
            objective=objective,
        )


def vehicle_return(vehicle_id: str, from_node: str, from_time: float, battery: float, data: Dict[str, object]) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    vehicle = data["vehicles"][vehicle_id]
    arc = data["travel"][(from_node, vehicle["end_node"])]
    end_time = from_time + as_float(arc["travel_min"])
    end_battery = battery - as_float(arc["empty_energy_kwh"])
    return end_time, end_battery


def pattern_signature(vehicle_sequences: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, str]]]) -> Tuple[Tuple[str, Tuple[Tuple[str, str], ...]], ...]:
    return tuple(sorted((vehicle_id, tuple(events)) for vehicle_id, events in vehicle_sequences.items()))


def build_pattern(
    group_id: str,
    pattern_id: str,
    data: Dict[str, object],
    vehicle_trips: Dict[str, List[TripPlan]],
) -> Optional[Pattern]:
    facilities = data["facilities"]
    vehicles = data["vehicles"]

    vehicle_sequences: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    facility_total_kg: Dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)
    facility_red_kg: Dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)
    facility_yellow_kg: Dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)
    facility_window_counts: Dict[Tuple[str, str], int] = defaultdict(int)
    facility_window_kg: Dict[Tuple[str, str], float] = defaultdict(float)
    final_times: Dict[str, float] = {}
    final_batteries: Dict[str, float] = {}

    objective = 0.0
    for vehicle_id, trips in vehicle_trips.items():
        events: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
        objective += as_float(vehicles[vehicle_id]["fixed_cost"])
        for trip in trips:
            for clinic_id in trip.clinic_order:
                events.append(("pickup", clinic_id))
            events.append(("unload", trip.facility_id))

            facility_total_kg[trip.facility_id] += trip.kg
            facility_red_kg[trip.facility_id] += trip.red_kg
            facility_yellow_kg[trip.facility_id] += trip.yellow_kg
            facility_window_counts[(trip.facility_id, trip.unload_window_id)] += 1
            facility_window_kg[(trip.facility_id, trip.unload_window_id)] += trip.kg
            objective += trip.objective

        vehicle_sequences[vehicle_id] = events
        last_trip = trips[-1]
        return_time, return_battery = vehicle_return(
            vehicle_id,
            facilities[last_trip.facility_id]["node_id"],
            last_trip.end_time,
            last_trip.end_battery,
            data,
        )
        if return_battery < -FLOAT_TOL:
            return None
        if return_time > as_float(vehicles[vehicle_id]["shift_end_min"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
            return None
        final_times[vehicle_id] = return_time
        final_batteries[vehicle_id] = return_battery

    return Pattern(
        group_id=group_id,
        pattern_id=pattern_id,
        vehicle_sequences=vehicle_sequences,
        objective=objective,
        final_times=final_times,
        final_batteries=final_batteries,
        facility_total_kg=dict(facility_total_kg),
        facility_red_kg=dict(facility_red_kg),
        facility_yellow_kg=dict(facility_yellow_kg),
        facility_window_counts=dict(facility_window_counts),
        facility_window_kg=dict(facility_window_kg),
        facilities_used=tuple(sorted(facility_total_kg)),
    )


def generate_partition_patterns(
    group_id: str,
    data: Dict[str, object],
    trip_solver: TripSolver,
    clinic_ids: Sequence[str],
    vehicle_a: str,
    vehicle_b: str,
    facility_id: str,
    limit: int,
) -> List[Pattern]:
    patterns: List[Pattern] = []
    all_mask = (1 << len(clinic_ids)) - 1
    pattern_idx = 0
    for mask in range(1, all_mask):
        if not (mask & 1):
            continue
        subset_a = [clinic_ids[i] for i in range(len(clinic_ids)) if (mask >> i) & 1]
        subset_b = [clinic_ids[i] for i in range(len(clinic_ids)) if not ((mask >> i) & 1)]
        if not subset_b:
            continue
        for assigned_a, assigned_b in ((subset_a, subset_b), (subset_b, subset_a)):
            trip_a = trip_solver.best_trip(
                vehicle_a,
                data["vehicles"][vehicle_a]["start_node"],
                as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_a]["shift_start_min"]),
                assigned_a,
                facility_id,
                as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_a]["start_battery_kwh"]),
            )
            if trip_a is None:
                continue
            trip_b = trip_solver.best_trip(
                vehicle_b,
                data["vehicles"][vehicle_b]["start_node"],
                as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_b]["shift_start_min"]),
                assigned_b,
                facility_id,
                as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_b]["start_battery_kwh"]),
            )
            if trip_b is None:
                continue
            pattern = build_pattern(
                group_id,
                f"{group_id}_{pattern_idx}",
                data,
                {vehicle_a: [trip_a], vehicle_b: [trip_b]},
            )
            if pattern is not None:
                patterns.append(pattern)
                pattern_idx += 1
    return keep_top_patterns(patterns, limit)

                        start_node_2,

                        second_subset,

def generate_north_central_patterns(
    data: Dict[str, object],
    trip_solver: TripSolver,
    limit: int,
) -> List[Pattern]:
    patterns: List[Pattern] = []
    vehicle_id = "EV8"
    pattern_idx = 0

    north_ids = data["zone_clinics"]["north"]
    central_ids = data["zone_clinics"]["central"]
    start_node = data["vehicles"][vehicle_id]["start_node"]
    start_time = as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_id]["shift_start_min"])
    start_battery = as_float(data["vehicles"][vehicle_id]["start_battery_kwh"])

    tasks = [
        ("north", north_ids, ["F5"]),
        ("central", central_ids, ["F1", "F3", "F5"]),
    ]
    for first_task, second_task in ((tasks[0], tasks[1]), (tasks[1], tasks[0])):
        for facility_1 in first_task[2]:
            trip_1 = trip_solver.best_trip(
                vehicle_id,
                start_node,
                start_time,
                first_task[1],
                facility_1,
                start_battery,
            )
            if trip_1 is None:
                continue
            start_node_2 = data["facilities"][facility_1]["node_id"]
            for facility_2 in second_task[2]:
                trip_2 = trip_solver.best_trip(
                    vehicle_id,
                    start_node_2,
                    trip_1.end_time,
                    second_task[1],
                    facility_2,
                    trip_1.end_battery,
                )
                if trip_2 is None:
                    continue
                pattern = build_pattern(
                    "north_central",
                    f"north_central_{pattern_idx}",
                    data,
                    {vehicle_id: [trip_1, trip_2]},
                )
                if pattern is not None:
                    patterns.append(pattern)
                    pattern_idx += 1
    return keep_top_patterns(patterns, limit)


def keep_top_patterns(patterns: List[Pattern], limit: int) -> List[Pattern]:
    dedup: Dict[Tuple[Tuple[str, Tuple[Tuple[str, str], ...]], ...], Pattern] = {}
    for pattern in patterns:
        signature = pattern_signature(pattern.vehicle_sequences)
        if signature not in dedup or (pattern.objective, max(pattern.final_times.values())) < (
            dedup[signature].objective,
            max(dedup[signature].final_times.values()),
        ):
            dedup[signature] = pattern
    kept = list(dedup.values())
    kept.sort(key=lambda p: (p.objective, max(p.final_times.values())))
    return kept[:limit]


def build_candidate_groups(data: Dict[str, object]) -> Dict[str, List[Pattern]]:
    trip_solver = TripSolver(data)

    groups = {
        "west_pair": generate_partition_patterns(
            "west_pair",
            data,
            trip_solver,
            data["zone_clinics"]["west"],
            "EV5",
            "EV7",
            "F1",
            limit=150,
        ),
        "south_pair": generate_partition_patterns(
            "south_pair",
            data,
            trip_solver,
            data["zone_clinics"]["south"],
            "EV3",
            "EV6",
            "F3",
            limit=150,
        ),
        "southwest": generate_two_trip_patterns(
            "southwest",
            data,
            trip_solver,
            "EV1",
            data["zone_clinics"]["southwest"],
            ["F1", "F3"],
            limit=300,
        ),
        "east": generate_two_trip_patterns(
            "east",
            data,
            trip_solver,
            "EV2",
            data["zone_clinics"]["east"],
            ["F1", "F2", "F5"],
            limit=300,
        ),
        "southeast": generate_two_trip_patterns(
            "southeast",
            data,
            trip_solver,
            "EV4",
            data["zone_clinics"]["southeast"],
            ["F2", "F5"],
            limit=250,
        ),
        "north_central": generate_north_central_patterns(data, trip_solver, limit=100),
    }
    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        if not patterns:
            raise RuntimeError(f"No feasible patterns generated for group {group_id}")
    return groups


def partial_capacity_feasible(
    selected_patterns: Sequence[Pattern],
    data: Dict[str, object],
) -> bool:
    facilities = data["facilities"]
    window_caps = {
        (row["facility_id"], row["window_id"]): (int(row["max_unloads"]), as_float(row["max_kg"]))
        for row in data["windows"]
    }

    facility_total = defaultdict(float)
    facility_red = defaultdict(float)
    facility_yellow = defaultdict(float)
    window_count = defaultdict(int)
    window_kg = defaultdict(float)

    for pattern in selected_patterns:
        for facility_id, kg in pattern.facility_total_kg.items():
            facility_total[facility_id] += kg
            facility_red[facility_id] += pattern.facility_red_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0)
            facility_yellow[facility_id] += pattern.facility_yellow_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0)
            if facility_total[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["daily_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
            if facility_red[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["red_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
            if facility_yellow[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["yellow_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
        for key, count in pattern.facility_window_counts.items():
            window_count[key] += count
            window_kg[key] += pattern.facility_window_kg[key]
            cap_count, cap_kg = window_caps[key]
            if window_count[key] > cap_count or window_kg[key] > cap_kg + FLOAT_TOL:
                return False
    return True


def find_feasible_combination(groups: Dict[str, List[Pattern]], data: Dict[str, object]) -> Dict[str, Pattern]:
    ordered_group_ids = sorted(groups, key=lambda group_id: len(groups[group_id]))
    best_solution: Dict[str, Pattern] = {}

    def backtrack(index: int, chosen: Dict[str, Pattern]) -> bool:
        if index == len(ordered_group_ids):
            best_solution.update(chosen)
            return True
        group_id = ordered_group_ids[index]
        for pattern in groups[group_id]:
            selected = list(chosen.values()) + [pattern]
            if not partial_capacity_feasible(selected, data):
                continue
            chosen[group_id] = pattern
            if backtrack(index + 1, chosen):
                return True
            del chosen[group_id]
        return False

    if not backtrack(0, {}):
        raise RuntimeError("No feasible incumbent found from candidate patterns")
    return best_solution


def solve_master(
    groups: Dict[str, List[Pattern]],
    data: Dict[str, object],
    incumbent: Dict[str, Pattern],
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Pattern], Dict[str, object]]:
    facilities = data["facilities"]
    windows = data["windows"]
    time_limit = MASTER_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS
    env_limit = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
    if env_limit is not None:
        time_limit = min(time_limit, float(env_limit))

    model = Model("medical_waste_pattern_master")
    model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)

    x_vars = {}
    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
            x_vars[(group_id, idx)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{group_id},{idx}]")

    y_vars = {facility_id: model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"y[{facility_id}]") for facility_id in facilities}

    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[(group_id, idx)] for idx in range(len(patterns))) == 1,
            name=f"choose_one[{group_id}]",
        )

    for facility_id, facility in facilities.items():
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()

                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["daily_capacity_kg"]),
            name=f"facility_total[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_red_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["red_capacity_kg"]),
            name=f"facility_red[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_yellow_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["yellow_capacity_kg"]),
            name=f"facility_yellow[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(facility["daily_capacity_kg"]) * y_vars[facility_id],
            name=f"open_link_upper[{facility_id}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            >= data["config"]["minimum_facility_throughput_kg"] * y_vars[facility_id],
            name=f"open_link_lower[{facility_id}]",
        )

    for row in windows:
        key = (row["facility_id"], row["window_id"])
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_window_counts.get(key, 0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= int(row["max_unloads"]),
            name=f"window_count[{key[0]},{key[1]}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern.facility_window_kg.get(key, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
                for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
                for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
            )
            <= as_float(row["max_kg"]),
            name=f"window_kg[{key[0]},{key[1]}]",
        )

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(y_vars[facility_id] for facility_id in facilities) <= int(data["config"]["max_open_facilities"]),
        name="max_open_facilities",
    )

    objective = quicksum(
        pattern.objective * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
        for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
    ) + quicksum(
        as_float(facility["open_cost"]) * y_vars[facility_id] for facility_id, facility in facilities.items()
    )
    model.setObjective(objective, "minimize")

    warm = model.createSol()
    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        incumbent_signature = pattern_signature(incumbent[group_id].vehicle_sequences)
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
            value = 1.0 if pattern_signature(pattern.vehicle_sequences) == incumbent_signature else 0.0
            model.setSolVal(warm, x_vars[(group_id, idx)], value)
    used_facilities = set()
    for pattern in incumbent.values():
        used_facilities.update(pattern.facilities_used)
    for facility_id in facilities:
        model.setSolVal(warm, y_vars[facility_id], 1.0 if facility_id in used_facilities else 0.0)
    model.addSol(warm, free=True)

    model.optimize()
    stats = {
        "status": str(model.getStatus()),
        "has_solution": model.getNSols() > 0,
        "best_bound": model.getDualbound() if model.getStage() >= 10 else None,
        "gap": model.getGap() if model.getNSols() > 0 else None,
        "time_limit": time_limit,
    }

    if model.getNSols() == 0:
        return incumbent, stats

    solution = model.getBestSol()
    chosen: Dict[str, Pattern] = {}
    for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
        picked_pattern = None
        for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
            if model.getSolVal(solution, x_vars[(group_id, idx)]) > 0.5:
                picked_pattern = pattern
                break
        if picked_pattern is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"No selected pattern found for group {group_id}")
        chosen[group_id] = picked_pattern

    stats["objective"] = model.getObjVal()
    return chosen, stats


def flatten_solution_rows(selected_patterns: Dict[str, Pattern], data: Dict[str, object]) -> List[List[str]]:
    clinics = data["clinics"]
    facilities = data["facilities"]
    vehicles = data["vehicles"]

    vehicle_events: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    for pattern in selected_patterns.values():
        for vehicle_id, events in pattern.vehicle_sequences.items():
            if vehicle_id in vehicle_events:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Vehicle {vehicle_id} assigned in multiple patterns")
            vehicle_events[vehicle_id] = events

    rows: List[List[str]] = []
    for vehicle_id in sorted(vehicle_events):
        sequence = 1
        rows.append(["start", vehicle_id, str(sequence), vehicles[vehicle_id]["start_node"], "", "", "", ""])
        sequence += 1
        for event_type, event_id in vehicle_events[vehicle_id]:
            if event_type == "pickup":
                rows.append(
                    ["pickup", vehicle_id, str(sequence), clinics[event_id]["node_id"], event_id, "", "", ""]
                )
            elif event_type == "unload":
                rows.append(
                    ["unload", vehicle_id, str(sequence), facilities[event_id]["node_id"], "", event_id, "", ""]
                )
            else:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Unsupported event type {event_type}")
            sequence += 1
        rows.append(["end", vehicle_id, str(sequence), vehicles[vehicle_id]["end_node"], "", "", "", ""])
    return rows


def write_solution(rows: List[List[str]]) -> None:
    with SOLUTION_PATH.open("w", newline="") as fh:
        writer = csv.writer(fh)
        writer.writerow(
            ["row_type", "vehicle_id", "sequence", "node_id", "clinic_id", "facility_id", "charger_id", "charge_kwh"]
        )
        writer.writerows(rows)


def audit_solution(solution_path: Path, data: Dict[str, object]) -> Dict[str, object]:
    rows = read_csv(solution_path)
    clinics = data["clinics"]
    vehicles = data["vehicles"]
    permits = data["permits"]
    facilities = data["facilities"]
    chargers = data["chargers"]
    nodes = data["nodes"]
    windows = data["windows"]
    windows_by_facility = data["windows_by_facility"]
    facility_rules = data["facility_rules"]
    incompatible_pairs = data["incompatible_pairs"]

    required_columns = [
        "row_type",
        "vehicle_id",
        "sequence",
        "node_id",
        "clinic_id",
        "facility_id",
        "charger_id",
        "charge_kwh",
    ]
    violations: List[str] = []
    if list(rows[0].keys()) != required_columns:
        violations.append("CSV header does not match required schema")

    per_vehicle_rows: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, str]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        if row["vehicle_id"] not in vehicles:
            violations.append(f"Unknown vehicle_id {row['vehicle_id']}")
            continue
        if row["node_id"] not in nodes:
            violations.append(f"Unknown node_id {row['node_id']}")
            continue
        per_vehicle_rows[row["vehicle_id"]].append(row)

    picked = set()
    facility_total = defaultdict(float)
    facility_red = defaultdict(float)
    facility_yellow = defaultdict(float)
    window_count = defaultdict(int)
    window_kg = defaultdict(float)
    station_sessions = defaultdict(int)
    station_kwh = defaultdict(float)
    objective = 0.0

    for vehicle_id, vehicle_rows in per_vehicle_rows.items():
        try:
            vehicle_rows.sort(key=lambda row: int(row["sequence"]))
        except ValueError:
            violations.append(f"Non-integer sequence on vehicle {vehicle_id}")
            continue
        expected_sequence = 1
        for row in vehicle_rows:
            if int(row["sequence"]) != expected_sequence:
                violations.append(f"Non-consecutive sequence on vehicle {vehicle_id}")
                break
            expected_sequence += 1
        if vehicle_rows[0]["row_type"] != "start":

0612:             "west_pair",

0615:             data["zone_clinics"]["west"],

0617:             "EV7",
0618:             "F1",

0641:             "east",

0644:             "EV2",

0648:         ),

0650:             "southeast",
0651:             data,
0652:             trip_solver,
0653:             "EV4",
0654:             data["zone_clinics"]["southeast"],
0655:             ["F2", "F5"],
0656:             limit=250,
0657:         ),
0658:         "north_central": generate_north_central_patterns(data, trip_solver, limit=100),
0659:     }
0660:     for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
0661:         if not patterns:
0662:             raise RuntimeError(f"No feasible patterns generated for group {group_id}")
0663:     return groups
0664:
0665:
0666: def partial_capacity_feasible(
0667:     selected_patterns: Sequence[Pattern],
0668:     data: Dict[str, object],
0669: ) -> bool:
0670:     facilities = data["facilities"]
0671:     window_caps = {
0672:         (row["facility_id"], row["window_id"]): (int(row["max_unloads"]), as_float(row["max_kg"]))
0673:         for row in data["windows"]
0674:     }
0675:
0676:     facility_total = defaultdict(float)
0677:     facility_red = defaultdict(float)
0678:     facility_yellow = defaultdict(float)
0679:     window_count = defaultdict(int)
0680:     window_kg = defaultdict(float)
0681:
0682:     for pattern in selected_patterns:
0683:         for facility_id, kg in pattern.facility_total_kg.items():
0684:             facility_total[facility_id] += kg
0685:             facility_red[facility_id] += pattern.facility_red_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0)
0686:             facility_yellow[facility_id] += pattern.facility_yellow_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0)
0687:             if facility_total[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["daily_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
0688:                 return False
0689:             if facility_red[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["red_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
0690:                 return False
0691:             if facility_yellow[facility_id] > as_float(facilities[facility_id]["yellow_capacity_kg"]) + FLOAT_TOL:
0692:                 return False
0693:         for key, count in pattern.facility_window_counts.items():
0694:             window_count[key] += count
0695:             window_kg[key] += pattern.facility_window_kg[key]
0696:             cap_count, cap_kg = window_caps[key]
0697:             if window_count[key] > cap_count or window_kg[key] > cap_kg + FLOAT_TOL:
0698:                 return False
0699:     return True
0700:
0701:
0702: def find_feasible_combination(groups: Dict[str, List[Pattern]], data: Dict[str, object]) -> Dict[str, Pattern]:
0703:     ordered_group_ids = sorted(groups, key=lambda group_id: len(groups[group_id]))
0704:     best_solution: Dict[str, Pattern] = {}
0705:
0706:     def backtrack(index: int, chosen: Dict[str, Pattern]) -> bool:
0707:         if index == len(ordered_group_ids):
0708:             best_solution.update(chosen)
0709:             return True
0710:         group_id = ordered_group_ids[index]
0711:         for pattern in groups[group_id]:
0712:             selected = list(chosen.values()) + [pattern]
0713:             if not partial_capacity_feasible(selected, data):
0714:                 continue
0715:             chosen[group_id] = pattern
0716:             if backtrack(index + 1, chosen):
0717:                 return True
0718:             del chosen[group_id]
0719:         return False
0720:
0721:     if not backtrack(0, {}):
0722:         raise RuntimeError("No feasible incumbent found from candidate patterns")
0723:     return best_solution
0724:
0725:
0726: def solve_master(
0727:     groups: Dict[str, List[Pattern]],
0728:     data: Dict[str, object],
0729:     incumbent: Dict[str, Pattern],
0730: ) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Pattern], Dict[str, object]]:
0731:     facilities = data["facilities"]
0732:     windows = data["windows"]
0733:     time_limit = MASTER_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS
0734:     env_limit = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
0735:     if env_limit is not None:
0736:         time_limit = min(time_limit, float(env_limit))
0737:
0738:     model = Model("medical_waste_pattern_master")
0739:     model.setParam("limits/gap", REL_GAP)
0740:     model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
0741:     model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
0742:
0743:     x_vars = {}
0744:     for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
0745:         for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
0746:             x_vars[(group_id, idx)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{group_id},{idx}]")
0747:
0748:     y_vars = {facility_id: model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"y[{facility_id}]") for facility_id in facilities}
0749:
0750:     for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
0751:         model.addCons(
0752:             quicksum(x_vars[(group_id, idx)] for idx in range(len(patterns))) == 1,
0753:             name=f"choose_one[{group_id}]",
0754:         )
0755:
0756:     for facility_id, facility in facilities.items():
0757:         model.addCons(
0758:             quicksum(
0759:                 pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0760:                 for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0761:                 for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0762:             )
0763:             <= as_float(facility["daily_capacity_kg"]),
0764:             name=f"facility_total[{facility_id}]",
0765:         )
0766:         model.addCons(
0767:             quicksum(
0768:                 pattern.facility_red_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0769:                 for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0770:                 for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0771:             )
0772:             <= as_float(facility["red_capacity_kg"]),
0773:             name=f"facility_red[{facility_id}]",
0774:         )
0775:         model.addCons(
0776:             quicksum(
0777:                 pattern.facility_yellow_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0778:                 for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0779:                 for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0780:             )
0781:             <= as_float(facility["yellow_capacity_kg"]),
0782:             name=f"facility_yellow[{facility_id}]",
0783:         )
0784:         model.addCons(
0785:             quicksum(
0786:                 pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0787:                 for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0788:                 for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0789:             )
0790:             <= as_float(facility["daily_capacity_kg"]) * y_vars[facility_id],
0791:             name=f"open_link_upper[{facility_id}]",
0792:         )
0793:         model.addCons(
0794:             quicksum(
0795:                 pattern.facility_total_kg.get(facility_id, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0796:                 for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0797:                 for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0798:             )
0799:             >= data["config"]["minimum_facility_throughput_kg"] * y_vars[facility_id],
0800:             name=f"open_link_lower[{facility_id}]",
0801:         )
0802:
0803:     for row in windows:
0804:         key = (row["facility_id"], row["window_id"])
0805:         model.addCons(
0806:             quicksum(
0807:                 pattern.facility_window_counts.get(key, 0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0808:                 for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0809:                 for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0810:             )
0811:             <= int(row["max_unloads"]),
0812:             name=f"window_count[{key[0]},{key[1]}]",
0813:         )
0814:         model.addCons(
0815:             quicksum(
0816:                 pattern.facility_window_kg.get(key, 0.0) * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0817:                 for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0818:                 for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0819:             )
0820:             <= as_float(row["max_kg"]),
0821:             name=f"window_kg[{key[0]},{key[1]}]",
0822:         )
0823:
0824:     model.addCons(
0825:         quicksum(y_vars[facility_id] for facility_id in facilities) <= int(data["config"]["max_open_facilities"]),
0826:         name="max_open_facilities",
0827:     )
0828:
0829:     objective = quicksum(
0830:         pattern.objective * x_vars[(group_id, idx)]
0831:         for group_id, patterns in groups.items()
0832:         for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns)
0833:     ) + quicksum(
0834:         as_float(facility["open_cost"]) * y_vars[facility_id] for facility_id, facility in facilities.items()
0835:     )
0836:     model.setObjective(objective, "minimize")
0837:
0838:     warm = model.createSol()
0839:     for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
0840:         incumbent_signature = pattern_signature(incumbent[group_id].vehicle_sequences)
0841:         for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
0842:             value = 1.0 if pattern_signature(pattern.vehicle_sequences) == incumbent_signature else 0.0
0843:             model.setSolVal(warm, x_vars[(group_id, idx)], value)
0844:     used_facilities = set()
0845:     for pattern in incumbent.values():
0846:         used_facilities.update(pattern.facilities_used)
0847:     for facility_id in facilities:
0848:         model.setSolVal(warm, y_vars[facility_id], 1.0 if facility_id in used_facilities else 0.0)
0849:     model.addSol(warm, free=True)
0850:
0851:     model.optimize()
0852:     stats = {
0853:         "status": str(model.getStatus()),
0854:         "has_solution": model.getNSols() > 0,
0855:         "best_bound": model.getDualbound() if model.getStage() >= 10 else None,
0856:         "gap": model.getGap() if model.getNSols() > 0 else None,
0857:         "time_limit": time_limit,
0858:     }
0859:
0860:     if model.getNSols() == 0:
0861:         return incumbent, stats
0862:
0863:     solution = model.getBestSol()
0864:     chosen: Dict[str, Pattern] = {}
0865:     for group_id, patterns in groups.items():
0866:         picked_pattern = None
0867:         for idx, pattern in enumerate(patterns):
0868:             if model.getSolVal(solution, x_vars[(group_id, idx)]) > 0.5:
0869:                 picked_pattern = pattern
0870:                 break
0871:         if picked_pattern is None:
0872:             raise RuntimeError(f"No selected pattern found for group {group_id}")
0873:         chosen[group_id] = picked_pattern
0874:
0875:     stats["objective"] = model.getObjVal()
0876:     return chosen, stats

0879: def flatten_solution_rows(selected_patterns: Dict[str, Pattern], data: Dict[str, object]) -> List[List[str]]:
0880:     clinics = data["clinics"]
0881:     facilities = data["facilities"]

0895:         sequence += 1

0900:                 )

0904:                 )
0905:             else:

0907:             sequence += 1

    vrows.sort(key=lambda r:int(r['sequence']))
    time=float(data['vehicles'][vid]['shift_start_min']); battery=float(data['vehicles'][vid]['start_battery_kwh']); node=data['vehicles'][vid]['start_node']; red=yellow=0.0; used=False

        time,battery,cost=mod.advance_arc(node,row['node_id'],time,battery,red,yellow,data)
        obj += cost; node=row['node_id']
        if rt=='pickup':
            used=True
            clinic=data['clinics'][row['clinic_id']]
            earliest=float(clinic['earliest_min'])
            if time<earliest:
                obj += mod.wait_risk_cost(earliest-time,red,yellow,data)
                time=earliest
            service=float(clinic['service_min'])
            obj += mod.wait_risk_cost(service,red,yellow,data)
            time += service
            red += float(clinic['red_kg']); yellow += float(clinic['yellow_kg'])
        elif rt=='unload':
            used=True
            unload_time=time
            for window in data['windows_by_facility'][row['facility_id']]:
                s=float(window['start_min']); e=float(window['end_min'])
                if unload_time < s:
                    obj += mod.wait_risk_cost(s-unload_time,red,yellow,data)
                    unload_time = s
                if unload_time <= e + mod.FLOAT_TOL:
                    break
            rules=data['facility_rules'][row['facility_id']]
            obj += rules['red']['treatment_cost_per_kg']*red + rules['yellow']['treatment_cost_per_kg']*yellow
            time=unload_time; red=yellow=0.0
        elif rt=='charge':
            used=True
            amt=float(row['charge_kwh']); ch=data['chargers'][row['charger_id']]
            # queue and charging should only add risk if loaded, covered by wait_risk
            obj += mod.wait_risk_cost(float(ch['queue_delay_min']) + amt/float(ch['rate_kwh_per_min']), red, yellow, data)
            time += float(ch['queue_delay_min']) + amt/float(ch['rate_kwh_per_min'])
            battery += amt
            obj += amt*float(ch['energy_price'])
        elif rt=='end':
            pass
    if used:
        obj += float(data['vehicles'][vid]['fixed_cost'])
open_facilities=set(r['facility_id'] for r in rows if r['row_type']=='unload')

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import importlib.util, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('mw','/app/submissions/solve.py')
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=mod; spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
data=mod.load_data()
# monkey patch corrected build_pattern
orig_build = mod.build_pattern

def build_pattern_fixed(group_id, pattern_id, data, vehicle_trips):
    pat = orig_build(group_id, pattern_id, data, vehicle_trips)
    if pat is None:
        return None
    objective = pat.objective
    final_times = dict(pat.final_times)
    final_batteries = dict(pat.final_batteries)
    # recompute objective by adding empty return travel cost per vehicle
    for vehicle_id, trips in vehicle_trips.items():
        last = trips[-1]
        facility_node = data['facilities'][last.facility_id]['node_id']
        arc = data['travel'][(facility_node, data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['end_node'])]
        objective += float(data['config']['travel_cost_per_km']) * float(arc['distance_km'])
    pat.objective = objective
    return pat
mod.build_pattern = build_pattern_fixed
trip_solver=mod.TripSolver(data)

import importlib.util, sys, itertools, math, time
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('mw','/app/submissions/solve.py')
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=mod; spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
data=mod.load_data(); trip_solver=mod.TripSolver(data)
config=data['config']
# helper corrected trip total + return

def trip_obj(trip):
    return trip.objective

def return_cost(vehicle_id, facility_id):
    arc=data['travel'][(data['facilities'][facility_id]['node_id'], data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['end_node'])]
    return config['travel_cost_per_km'] * float(arc['distance_km'])

def facility_agg(trips):
    tot={}; red={}; yel={}; win={}; winkg={}
    for tr in trips:
        fid=tr.facility_id
        tot[fid]=tot.get(fid,0)+tr.kg
        red[fid]=red.get(fid,0)+tr.red_kg
        yel[fid]=yel.get(fid,0)+tr.yellow_kg
        key=(fid,tr.unload_window_id)
        win[key]=win.get(key,0)+1
        winkg[key]=winkg.get(key,0)+tr.kg
    return tot,red,yel,win,winkg

def feasible_agg(extra_trips, fixed_patterns):
    facilities=data['facilities']; windows={(r['facility_id'],r['window_id']):(int(r['max_unloads']),float(r['max_kg'])) for r in data['windows']}
    tot={}; red={}; yel={}; cnt={}; kg={}
    # fixed patterns from current incumbent except vehicle under test
    for pat in fixed_patterns:
        for fid,v in pat.facility_total_kg.items():
            tot[fid]=tot.get(fid,0)+v
            red[fid]=red.get(fid,0)+pat.facility_red_kg.get(fid,0)
            yel[fid]=yel.get(fid,0)+pat.facility_yellow_kg.get(fid,0)
        for k,v in pat.facility_window_counts.items():
            cnt[k]=cnt.get(k,0)+v
            kg[k]=kg.get(k,0)+pat.facility_window_kg.get(k,0)
    a,b,c,d,e=facility_agg(extra_trips)
    for fid,v in a.items():
        tot[fid]=tot.get(fid,0)+v
        red[fid]=red.get(fid,0)+b.get(fid,0)
        yel[fid]=yel.get(fid,0)+c.get(fid,0)
    for k,v in d.items():
        cnt[k]=cnt.get(k,0)+v
        kg[k]=kg.get(k,0)+e.get(k,0)
    for fid,v in tot.items():
        fac=facilities[fid]
        if v > float(fac['daily_capacity_kg']) + 1e-8: return False
        if red.get(fid,0) > float(fac['red_capacity_kg']) + 1e-8: return False
        if yel.get(fid,0) > float(fac['yellow_capacity_kg']) + 1e-8: return False
    for k,v in cnt.items():
        lim=windows[k]
        if v > lim[0] or kg[k] > lim[1] + 1e-8: return False
    return True

# build fixed incumbent patterns using current candidate groups with corrected objective as before
orig_build = mod.build_pattern

def build_pattern_fixed(group_id, pattern_id, data, vehicle_trips):
    pat = orig_build(group_id, pattern_id, data, vehicle_trips)
    if pat is None: return None
    for vehicle_id, trips in vehicle_trips.items():
        last=trips[-1]
        pat.objective += return_cost(vehicle_id,last.facility_id)
    return pat
mod.build_pattern = build_pattern_fixed
groups=mod.build_candidate_groups(data)
inc=mod.find_feasible_combination(groups,data)
chosen,stats=mod.solve_master(groups,data,inc)
print('baseline',stats['objective'])
veh_to_group={}
for gid,pat in chosen.items():
    for vid in pat.vehicle_sequences: veh_to_group[vid]=gid
fixed_except={vid:[p for g,p in chosen.items() if g"'!=veh_to_group[vid]] for vid in veh_to_group}

# generate ordered nonempty 3-partitions

def ordered_three_parts(items):
    n=len(items)
    res=[]
    for assign in itertools.product(range(3), repeat=n):
        if set(assign)!={0,1,2}: continue
        parts=[[],[],[]]
        for i,a in enumerate(assign): parts[a].append(items[i])
        res.append(tuple(tuple(p) for p in parts))
    return res

for vehicle_id, clinic_ids, fac_choices in [
    ('"'EV2', data['zone_clinics']['east'], ['F2','F5','F1']),
    ('EV4', data['zone_clinics']['southeast'], ['F2','F5']),
]:
    start=time.time(); best=None; count=0
    parts=ordered_three_parts(clinic_ids)
    print(vehicle_id,'partitions',len(parts))
    for p1,p2,p3 in parts:
        for f1 in fac_choices:
            t1=trip_solver.best_trip(vehicle_id,data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['start_node'],float(data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['shift_start_min']),p1,f1,float(data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['start_battery_kwh']))
            if t1 is None: continue
            node1=data['facilities'][f1]['node_id']
            for f2 in fac_choices:
                t2=trip_solver.best_trip(vehicle_id,node1,t1.end_time,p2,f2,t1.end_battery)
                if t2 is None: continue
                node2=data['facilities'][f2]['node_id']
                for f3 in fac_choices:
                    t3=trip_solver.best_trip(vehicle_id,node2,t2.end_time,p3,f3,t2.end_battery)
                    if t3 is None: continue
                    rt, rb = mod.vehicle_return(vehicle_id,data['facilities'][f3]['node_id'],t3.end_time,t3.end_battery,data)
                    if rb < -1e-8 or rt > float(data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['shift_end_min']) + 1e-8: continue
                    trips=[t1,t2,t3]
                    if not feasible_agg(trips, fixed_except[vehicle_id]):
                        continue
                    obj=float(data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['fixed_cost']) + sum(t.objective for t in trips) + return_cost(vehicle_id,f3)
                    count += 1
                    if best is None or obj < best[0]:
                        best=(obj,[t.clinic_order for t in trips],[t.facility_id for t in trips],[t.unload_window_id for t in trips],rt)
    print(vehicle_id,'feasible3',count,'best',best,'secs',time.time()-start)

from pathlib import Path
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('mw','/app/submissions/solve.py')
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); sys.modules[spec.name]=mod; spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
data=mod.load_data(); trip_solver=mod.TripSolver(data)
config=data['config']
orig_build=mod.build_pattern

def return_cost(vehicle_id, facility_id):
    arc=data['travel'][(data['facilities'][facility_id]['node_id'], data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['end_node'])]
    return config['travel_cost_per_km'] * float(arc['distance_km'])

def build_pattern_fixed(group_id, pattern_id, data, vehicle_trips):
    pat=orig_build(group_id, pattern_id, data, vehicle_trips)
    if pat is None: return None
    for vehicle_id,trips in vehicle_trips.items():
        pat.objective += return_cost(vehicle_id, trips[-1].facility_id)
    return pat
mod.build_pattern=build_pattern_fixed

def generate_three_trip_patterns(group_id, vehicle_id, clinic_ids, facility_choices, limit):
    patterns=[]; idx=0
    start_node=data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['start_node']; start_time=float(data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['shift_start_min']); start_bat=float(data['vehicles'][vehicle_id]['start_battery_kwh'])
    n=len(clinic_ids)
    for assign in itertools.product(range(3), repeat=n):
        if set(assign)"'!={0,1,2}: continue
        parts=[[],[],[]]
        for i,a in enumerate(assign): parts[a].append(clinic_ids[i])
        for f1 in facility_choices:

            if t1 is None: continue
            n1=data['"'facilities'][f1]['node_id']
            for f2 in facility_choices:
                t2=trip_solver.best_trip(vehicle_id,n1,t1.end_time,parts[1],f2,t1.end_battery)
                if t2 is None: continue
                n2=data['facilities'][f2]['node_id']
                for f3 in facility_choices:
                    t3=trip_solver.best_trip(vehicle_id,n2,t2.end_time,parts[2],f3,t2.end_battery)
                    if t3 is None: continue
                    pat=mod.build_pattern(group_id,f'{group_id}_3_{idx}',data,{vehicle_id:[t1,t2,t3]})
                    if pat is not None:
                        patterns.append(pat); idx += 1
    return mod.keep_top_patterns(patterns, limit)
